  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上非厄米简并通过谱合并讨论,但纠缠是本征矢量的性质,不能仅由本征值确定。现有研究缺乏将谱简并与纠缠性质分离的系统框架,需要建立新模型来独立探讨这两个概念,并理解非厄米参数如何在保持谱结构的同时影响量子态的纠缠演化。

Method: 构建紧凑二分系统模型:通过可逆的非幺正相似变换将具有简并本征空间的厄米算符转化为非厄米哈密顿量。以可精确求解的双量子比特系统为例进行分析。区分正定的右态约化密度矩阵与通常非正定的双正交约化。计算封闭形式的部分转置负性和Schmidt规范非局域魔力。在更大的二分空间中,使用Page熵和Haar平均纯度作为本征态典型性的参考值,建立不依赖于基依赖谱量的诊断方法。

Result: 双量子比特系统中,右本征态随非厄米参数从乘积态连续演化到最大纠缠态,但谱保持不变。纠缠随非厄米参数单调增长。非局域魔力在乘积态和最大纠缠态极限处消失,在中间耦合处达到最大值。成功获得部分转置负性和非局域魔力的解析解。发展的诊断方法能够有效分离非缺陷简并、例外点敏感性、Haar典型纠缠和非稳定子关联。

Conclusion: 本文成功将非厄米系统的谱简并与纠缠性质分离,证明在保持谱结构不变的条件下,非厄米性可以驱动量子态的纠缠演化。提出的诊断方法为理解非厄米量子系统的纠缠、魔力和典型性提供了新框架,无需依赖大量基依赖的谱量,为非厄米量子系统的纠缠动力学研究提供了新视角和工具。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian degeneracies are usually discussed through spectral coalescence, whereas entanglement is a property of eigenvectors and need not be fixed by the eigenvalues alone. We formulate a compact bipartite model that separates these two notions. A Hermitian operator with a degenerate eigenspace is transformed by an invertible non-unitary similarity map. The resulting Hamiltonian is non-Hermitian and retains a non-defective degeneracy at every finite value of the non-Hermiticity parameter. For an exactly solvable two-qubit realization, the right eigenstates evolve continuously from product states to maximally entangled states although the spectrum is unchanged. We distinguish the positive right-state reduced density matrix from the generally non-positive biorthogonal reduction, for which entropy may become complex. The same two-qubit solution gives a closed partial-transpose negativity and a Schmidt-gauged non-local magic. Entanglement grows monotonically with the non-Hermiticity parameter, whereas the non-local magic vanishes for both the product and maximally entangled limits and is largest at an intermediate coupling. In larger bipartite spaces, the Page entropy and Haar-averaged purity provide reference values for eigenstate typicality. These diagnostics separate non-defective degeneracy, exceptional-point sensitivity, Haar-typical entanglement, and non-stabilizer correlations without relying on a proliferation of basis-dependent spectral quantities.

</details>


### [106] [Deep Holes in the Clifford Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08403)
*Ian Teixeira,David Meyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了单量子比特Clifford层次结构在SU(2)中的拓扑闭包的覆盖半径为arccos√(5/6),证明了极值点(深洞)在Clifford门作用下形成192个元素的单一轨道,并通过将问题约化到SO(3)上的ℓ∞范数极小极大问题来解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中单量子比特Clifford层次结构的拓扑闭包是18个大圆(Clifford-Pauli圆)的并集,确定其覆盖半径对于理解量子门逼近和Clifford保真度具有重要意义。需要找到距离该闭包最远的点(深洞)及其几何结构,以刻画单量子比特酉算子的全层级Clifford保真度的最小值。

Method: 利用两个关键数学结构来解决覆盖问题:(1)将18个R^4平面的中心秩二投影算子构造成不可约SO(4)模Sym_0(4)的正交基;(2)利用单位四元数的投影轮廓恰好是其在双覆盖映射SU(2)→SO(3)下的像。通过这些结构将覆盖半径问题约化为SO(3)上关于ℓ∞范数的极小极大问题,并精确求解该问题同时对等号情形进行分类。

Result: 证明了单量子比特Clifford层次结构拓扑闭包的覆盖半径精确值为arccos√(5/6)。极值点(深洞)在Clifford门的左右乘法作用下形成大小为192的单一轨道,并给出了这些深洞的闭形式描述。等价地,单量子比特酉算子的全层级Clifford保真度的最小值为5/6。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了单量子比特Clifford层次结构在SU(2)中的覆盖半径问题,确定了覆盖半径为arccos√(5/6),最小Clifford保真度为5/6。通过建立与SO(4)不可约表示和SU(2)→SO(3)双覆盖映射的联系,将几何覆盖问题成功约化为可精确求解的优化问题,并完整刻画了深洞的轨道结构,为量子门逼近理论提供了精确的几何和代数刻画。

Abstract: We determine the covering radius of the topological closure of the single-qubit Clifford hierarchy in $\SU(2)\cong S^3$. This closure is a union of $18$ great circles --- the Clifford--Pauli circles --- and we prove that its covering radius is $\arccos\sqrt{5/6}$. The extremal points, which we call \emph{deep holes}, form a single orbit of size $192$ under left and right multiplication by Clifford gates, and are described in closed form. Equivalently, the minimum over one-qubit unitaries of the all-level Clifford fidelity is $5/6$. The proof rests on two structures attached to the configuration of $18$ planes in $\R^4$: their centered rank-two projectors form an orthonormal basis of the irreducible $\SO(4)$-module $\Sym_0(4)$, and the projection profile of a unit quaternion is exactly its image under the double cover $\SU(2)\to\SO(3)$. These reduce the covering problem to a minimax statement for the $\ell^\infty$-norm on $\SO(3)$ which we solve exactly, classifying its equality cases.

</details>


### [107] [Optimal strategies for shadow tomography with limited resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08429)
*Zhen-Peng Xu,Qi Ye,Jie Wang,Gereon Koßmann,Andreas Winter,René Schwonnek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究影子层析成像中泡利可观测量的最优测量策略,通过分析泡利反对易关系的挫折图参数,在有限交互范围约束下构造了最优无记忆测量协议,并证明Clifford测量在多种情况下达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影子层析分析虽然展现出显著的尺度优势,但实现这些保证的最优策略尚不明确,且所需测量在当前硬件上实施具有挑战性。针对泡利可观测量,需要在现实资源约束下计算最优样本复杂度参数并构造可实现的最优测量策略。

Method: 将问题转化为分析编码泡利反对易关系的挫折图(frustration graph)的图参数。采用无记忆协议(每个副本仅测量一次)和有界交互范围的测量约束。对一般情况提供高效数值方法,并针对完美图、单量子比特和双量子比特等场景解析证明Clifford测量的最优性。

Result: 成功证明Clifford测量在多种情况下是最优的,包括所有完美图、所有单量子比特测量场景、所有双量子比特测量场景等。将框架应用于哈密顿量能量估计,在分子基准测试中获得了构造性策略和改进的方差界。

Conclusion: 通过挫折图分析框架,为泡利可观测量的影子层析提供了理论最优且可实际实现的测量策略。Clifford测量在广泛场景下的最优性为当前量子硬件上的高效量子态表征提供了理论支撑和实用方案,特别是在分子哈密顿量能量估计等应用中展现出实际价值。

Abstract: Shadow tomography addresses the task of efficiently predicting many expectation values of an unknown quantum state from randomized measurements on comparatively few copies. Existing analyses promise large scaling advantages, but the optimal strategies realizing these guarantees are not always known, and the required measurements are potentially challenging to implement on current hardware. We address this gap for Pauli observables by computing optimal sample-complexity parameters and constructing optimal measurement strategies under realistic resource constraints. We focus on memoryless protocols, where each copy is measured only once, and on measurements with bounded interaction range. Our approach reduces the problem to the analysis of graph parameters of the frustration graph encoding the Pauli anticommutation relations. We provide efficient numerical methods for the general case and analytically prove that Clifford measurements are optimal in many situations. This includes all perfect graphs, all single-qubit, all two-qubit measurement scenarios, and more. Applied to Hamiltonian energy estimation, our framework yields constructive strategies and improved variance bounds for molecular benchmarks.

</details>


### [108] [The Input Problem: A Permanent Bottleneck for Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08433)
*Muhammad Faryad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子算法中经典数据输入编码的隐藏成本问题，指出标准编码方法（基态编码、幅度编码、Grover-Rudolph分布加载）的Θ(N)复杂度是理论限制而非工程问题，这一成本消除了量子振幅估计等算法的二次加速优势，并对量子机器学习的实际优势提出了严格的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法研究中普遍存在一个被忽视的问题：经典数据输入到量子态的编码成本往往被"免费提供"的假设所掩盖，而这一成本实际上经常超过算法本身的开销。这种会计疏漏导致对量子算法实际加速效果的高估，特别是在量子机器学习和蒙特卡洛积分等应用中。因此需要系统性地分析和量化数据加载的真实成本，重新评估量子算法的实际优势。

Method: 1) 系统性地分析三种标准量子编码方法（基态编码、幅度编码、Grover-Rudolph分布加载）转译到硬件门集后的实际成本；2) 在n=8量子比特的代表性加载任务上测量实际门操作数量，并分析其随量子比特数增长的规律；3) 将数据加载成本纳入量子振幅估计用于蒙特卡洛积分的复杂度分析；4) 分析量子机器学习中量子核方法的Gram矩阵制备成本及其摊销问题；5) 探讨可有效制备的量子态、设备生成分布、变分学习加载和摊销制备等替代方案；6) 提供可执行笔记本复现所有构造和测量结果。

Result: 实验测量显示，在n=8量子比特时，最优库实现需要247个CNOT门，每增加一个量子比特门数翻倍；经典预处理需要读取整个输入向量来生成旋转角度。数据加载的Θ(N)成本完全消除了量子振幅估计在蒙特卡洛积分中的二次加速优势。对于量子机器学习，强输入模型使得量子算法在经典数据上快速运行的同时也使经典去量子化成为可能；量子核方法承担Θ(M²)的态制备成本用于构建Gram矩阵，且该成本无法摊销。研究确认Θ(N)界限是计数定理而非可通过硬件改进消除的工程限制。

Conclusion: 量子算法在处理经典数据时，数据加载成本是不可忽视的本质性开销，其Θ(N)复杂度是理论限制而非硬件缺陷。这一成本严重制约了量子机器学习的实际优势：只有使用可高效制备的量子态、设备生成的分布、变分学习加载或摊销制备策略，才可能从量子机器学习中获得真正的优势。该研究为评估依赖输入的量子优势声明提供了检查清单，强调在评估量子算法性能时必须将完整的数据加载成本纳入考量，避免对量子加速效果的过度乐观估计。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are conventionally presented with their input state supplied for free. When the input is classical data, this convention conceals a cost that is frequently larger than the algorithm it precedes. We review what the three standard encodings, such as basis encoding, amplitude encoding, and Grover--Rudolph distribution loading, actually cost once transpiled to a hardware gate set, and argue that the resulting $Θ(N)$ bound is a counting theorem rather than an engineering limitation that improved hardware will remove. Measured gate counts for a representative loading task are reported: an optimal library implementation requires $247$ CNOT gates at $n=8$ qubits and doubles with each additional qubit, while the classical preprocessing that produces the rotation angles requires reading the entire input vector. We show how this cost eliminates the quadratic advantage of quantum amplitude estimation for Monte Carlo integration, and argue that the same accounting constrains quantum machine learning more broadly: the strong input models that make quantum algorithms fast on classical data also enable classical dequantization, and quantum kernel methods carry a $Θ(M^2)$ state-preparation cost for the Gram matrix that does not amortize. We explain that the efficiently preparable states, device-generated distributions, variationally learned loading, and amortized preparation are required to get advantage from quantum machine learning and close with a checklist for evaluating input-dependent advantage claims. Executable notebooks reproducing every construction and measurement discussed here are available.

</details>


### [109] [Spread of Entanglement in Generalized Kicked Ising Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09695)
*Tanay Pathak,Hiromi Ebisu,Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义kicked Ising链中高局域维度系统的纠缠动力学，发现了"对偶幺正"点的存在条件，并通过转移矩阵方法和replica技巧推导出q=3系统纠缠熵增长的精确解析表达式，证明了在对偶幺正点处纠缠熵随时间线性增长直至达到由子系统尺寸决定的最大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准kicked Ising链模型仅局限于qubit情况(局域维度q=2)，而将其推广到更高局域维度(q>2)可以探索更丰富的量子多体系统纠缠动力学行为。利用时空对偶性可以获得精确解析解的"对偶幺正"点，为研究高维度系统的纠缠演化提供了理论工具，但这类特殊点在不同维度下的存在性及其性质尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 将kicked Ising链模型从局域维度q=2推广到任意q>2的情况；2) 通过分析矩阵元条件识别不同局域维度下"对偶幺正"点的存在性，证明q=3和q=4存在少数解析对偶幺正点，而q≥5不存在；3) 针对高维度系统改进转移矩阵方法和replica技巧；4) 从一类可解初态出发，推导q=3(kicked Potts型)模型的纠缠熵增长精确表达式；5) 分析对偶幺正点处Rényi熵和von Neumann熵的时间演化行为。

Result: 发现q=3和q=4的系统存在少数解析的对偶幺正点，但q≥5由于缺乏满足矩阵元条件的唯一kicking强度而不存在这类特殊点。对于q=3的kicked Potts型模型，在对偶幺正点处，Rényi纠缠熵和von Neumann纠缠熵都表现出随时间线性增长的行为，直到达到由子系统尺寸所决定的饱和最大值。推导出的精确解析表达式完整描述了从可解初态出发的纠缠演化过程。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将kicked Ising链推广到高局域维度，揭示了对偶幺正点的存在性依赖于局域维度的限制条件(仅在q=3和q=4存在，q≥5不存在)。通过改进的解析方法获得了q=3系统纠缠熵演化的精确解，证实了对偶幺正点处纠缠的线性增长和饱和行为，为理解高维量子多体系统的纠缠动力学提供了新的理论框架和精确可解范例。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of entanglement in a generalized version of the kicked Ising chain, extending the model from the standard qubit case (local dimension $q=2$) to higher local dimensions ($q > 2$). We identify the existence of ''dual-unitary'' points where the model's space-time duality allows for exact analytical solutions. Our analysis reveals that while a few unique dual-unitary points exist analytically for systems with local dimensions $q=3$ and $q=4$, such points do not exist for $q \ge 5$ due to the lack of a unique kicking strength that satisfies the required matrix element conditions. Utilizing the transfer matrix method and a replica trick specifically adapted for higher dimensions, we derive exact expressions for the growth of entanglement entropy in the $q=3$ (kicked Potts-type) model starting from a class of solvable initial states. Our results demonstrate that at the dual-unitary point, both Rényi and von Neumann entanglement entropies grow linearly with time until reaching a maximum value determined by the subsystem size.

</details>


### [110] [Effect of Strong Field Space-time Features on Vacuum Pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08464)
*B. An,N. S. Lin,C. K. Li,M. Jiang,Y. J. Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用计算量子场论(CQFT)研究了束缚态在不同惯性参考系中的相对论动力学，发现通过参考系变换可以降低真空电子-正电子对产生所需的激光强度阈值，并建立了相对论效应主导量子真空衰变过程的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究中束缚态的时空性质和真空电子-正电子对产生需要极高的激光强度阈值。本文旨在探索利用相对论参考系变换来调控束缚态时空特性，从而降低真空对产生的激光强度阈值，为实验实现提供理论依据。

Method: 采用计算量子场论(CQFT)方法，研究束缚态在不同惯性参考系中的相对论动力学行为。通过解析和数值计算，分析四动量的洛伦兹变换如何重塑相空间中的真空激发路径，建立相对论效应与量子真空衰变过程之间的内在联系。

Result: 研究揭示了束缚态在给定势阱中的时空性质严格依赖于参考系。通过外场的时空调制可以显著降低真空电子-正电子对产生所需的激光强度阈值。解析和数值计算证明，这种阈值降低源于四动量的洛伦兹变换对相空间真空激发路径的重塑作用。

Conclusion: 通过发展计算量子场论框架，本文建立了一个相对论效应内在主导量子真空衰变过程的综合理论体系。研究证明利用参考系变换进行时空调制是降低真空对产生激光强度阈值的有效途径，为强场量子电动力学实验提供了新的理论指导和可行方案。

Abstract: The relativistic dynamics of bound states across inertial reference frames are investigated using the computational quantum field theory (CQFT). The results reveal that the spatiotemporal properties of bound states within a given potential well are strictly frame-dependent. Crucially, this spatiotemporal modulation of the external field enables a reduction in the laser intensity threshold required for vacuum electron-positron pair creation. Analytical and numerical calculations demonstrate that this threshold reduction originates from the Lorentz transformation of the four-momentum, which reshapes the vacuum excitation pathways in phase space. By developing CQFT, we establish a comprehensive framework in which relativistic effects intrinsically govern the quantum vacuum decay process

</details>


### [111] [Levitated Milligram-scale Ferromagnetic Magnetometer at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08544)
*Yuanji Sheng,Kenan Tian,Rui Li,Yiming Chen,Dingjiang Long,Siwen Chen,Peiran Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种室温下工作的毫克级抗磁悬浮铁磁体系统,通过优化几何结构和多通道耗散控制,在100 Hz频段实现了23 fT/√Hz的磁灵敏度,为生物磁场检测和超标准模型力探测建立了高性能磁力计平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬浮机械振子是新兴的超灵敏传感器,在应用和基础物理学中具有巨大潜力。悬浮铁磁体由于内部自旋噪声被快速平均,有望实现超高磁灵敏度。然而,现有系统在室温条件下实现高灵敏度磁测量仍面临挑战,需要开发能够在实用环境中工作的高性能磁力计。

Method: 采用毫克级抗磁悬浮铁磁体系统,在室温条件下运行。通过优化几何结构设计和实施多通道耗散控制技术来提升系统性能。利用铁磁材料的内部自旋噪声快速平均特性,结合悬浮机械振子的超灵敏特性构建磁力计平台。

Result: 在100 Hz频段实现了23 fT/√Hz(飞特斯拉每根号赫兹)的磁灵敏度。通过适度的技术改进,预期可以达到亚飞特斯拉(sub-fT)级别的灵敏度。该系统成功在室温环境下运行,具有实用化潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了室温工作的高灵敏度抗磁悬浮铁磁体磁力计系统,达到了飞特斯拉级磁灵敏度。该平台为生物磁场检测(如心磁图、脑磁图等)和超越标准模型的新物理力搜索提供了高性能测量工具,在基础物理研究和生物医学应用领域具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Levitated mechanical oscillators are emerging ultrasensitive sensors with tremendous potential in both applied and fundamental physics. Levitated ferromagnets, with internal spin noises rapidly averaged, promise ultrahigh magnetic sensitivity. Here, we demonstrate a milligram-scale diamagnetically levitated ferromagnet system operating at room temperature. Through optimized geometry and multi-channel dissipation control, we achieve a magnetic sensitivity of 23~fT$/\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ at frequency of 100-Hz level. We anticipate that a ferromagnetic magnetometer with subfemtotesla sensitivity is within reach, after modest technical improvements. This platform establishes a high-performance magnetometer for biomagnetic field detection and beyond-standard-model force searches.

</details>


### [112] [Quantized Stabilizer-Rényi Boundary Response across Fermionic SPT Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09749)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由费米子BDI链中开边界上对称保护的Majorana模式在稳定子Rényi熵中的表现,在Rényi指数α=1/2时发现了一个扣除体贡献后的边界响应,该响应在质量反转时趋近于|Δω|ln2(Δω为拓扑缠绕数变化),并且对体变形和局域边界扰动具有鲁棒性,可用于计数改变费米子SPT指数的Majorana通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对称保护拓扑相(SPT)中,开边界承载受对称性保护的Majorana零模,但其在稳定子Rényi熵中的特征信号被非普适的体积律贡献所掩盖。现有研究需要一种方法来隔离和识别边界上Majorana模式对熵的贡献,从而建立拓扑不变量与可观测量之间的直接联系。本文旨在通过特定的Rényi指数和扣除体贡献的方法,提取出能够反映费米子SPT拓扑指数变化的边界响应信号。

Method: 本文采用多种互补的解析和数值方法研究自由费米子BDI对称类链:(1)利用有限开链的精确关系式推导Rényi熵的表达式;(2)在Rényi指数α=1/2处进行大系统尺寸L极限下的Pfaffian行列式计算;(3)执行直接的正权重检验;(4)定义并计算扣除体积律贡献后的边界响应量;(5)通过质量参数反转跨越拓扑相变,追踪拓扑缠绕数Δω的变化;(6)测试该边界响应在原始体变形、局域边界扰动和破缺体对偶性下的稳定性。

Result: 在Rényi指数α=1/2时,成功提取出扣除体贡献后的边界响应信号。该响应在系统经历质量反转的拓扑相变时,数值上趋近于|Δω|ln2,其中Δω是系统拓扑缠绕数的变化量,ln2是单个Majorana零模的特征熵贡献。数值计算和解析推导相互验证了这一结果。进一步测试表明,该边界响应对原始的体哈密顿量变形、所测试的局域边界扰动以及体对偶对称性的破缺都表现出鲁棒性,证实了其作为拓扑不变量指示器的可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在自由费米子BDI链的Rényi指数α=1/2的稳定子熵中,扣除体积律后的边界响应能够准确捕获拓扑相变中Majorana通道数的变化,该响应量|Δω|ln2直接关联费米子SPT指数的改变。这一边界响应对多种微扰具有鲁棒性,为利用量子纠缠熵测量手段探测和表征对称保护拓扑相中的Majorana边界模式提供了新的理论工具和实验可观测量,在所研究的系统族中建立了拓扑不变量与可测量熵之间的定量关系。

Abstract: Open boundaries host symmetry-protected Majorana modes, yet their imprint on the stabilizer Rényi entropy is obscured by a nonuniversal volume law. At Rényi index $α=1/2$, we isolate a bulk-subtracted boundary response in free-fermion BDI chains using exact finite-open-chain relations, large-$L$ Pfaffian evaluations, and direct positive-weight checks. Across a mass inversion, this response approaches $|Δω|\ln 2$, where $Δω$ is the change in winding number. The response survives primitive bulk deformations, the tested local boundary perturbations, and broken bulk duality. Thus, for the families studied here, the boundary response counts the Majorana channels that change the fermionic SPT index.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum discord of Ganssian states in an expanding universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08603)
*Siwei Li,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了膨胀宇宙框架下连续变量量子失谐的重新分配现象,发现量子失谐对时空膨胀率比膨胀体积更敏感,且初始失谐会衰减并在其他模式对中诱导产生新的失谐,展现出全局重分配特性,为膨胀时空中的量子关联研究提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对膨胀宇宙中量子关联(特别是连续变量量子失谐)的动力学行为及其在不同模式对间的重新分配机制理解不足,需要系统研究时空膨胀如何影响量子失谐的演化和分布,以及如何利用量子失谐提取宇宙膨胀信息。

Method: 在膨胀宇宙框架下建立连续变量量子失谐的理论模型,分析双玻色模式系统中量子失谐对膨胀率和膨胀体积的依赖关系,计算时空膨胀诱导的不同模式对(跨观察者玻色-反玻色对、同观察者玻色-反玻色对、反玻色模式对)之间的失谐分布,并研究粒子动量和质量对提取宇宙膨胀信息的影响。

Result: 量子失谐对时空膨胀率的敏感性强于对膨胀体积的敏感性;初始量子失谐随膨胀率和膨胀体积增加而衰减;时空膨胀在额外模式对中诱导产生新的量子失谐,呈现全局重分配;跨观察者玻色-反玻色对的诱导失谐最大,同观察者玻色-反玻色对次之,反玻色模式对最小;低动量和最优质量的粒子是提取宇宙膨胀信息的更有利候选者。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了膨胀时空中连续变量量子失谐的重新分配规律,证明了量子失谐可作为探测宇宙膨胀的有效工具,显著丰富了膨胀时空中量子失谐的理论框架,为后续利用量子关联研究宇宙学问题提供了新视角和坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the redistribution of continuous-variable quantum discord within the framework of an expanding universe. We find that quantum discord exhibits stronger sensitivity to the spacetime expansion rate than to the expansion volume. As both the expansion rate and expansion volume increase, the initial quantum discord shared by the two bosonic modes decays, while quantum discord is induced in additional mode pairs by the underlying spacetime expansion, signaling a global redistribution of quantum correlations across the system. Specifically, the induced discord is largest for cross-observer bosonic-antibosonic pairs, followed by same-observer bosonic-antibosonic pairs, and smallest for the pair of antibosonic modes. Furthermore, our quantum discord analysis demonstrates that particles with lower momentum and optimal mass serve as more favorable candidates for extracting information about the expanding universe. This work substantially enriches the theoretical framework of quantum discord in expanding spacetimes, and provides new perspectives as well as a solid theoretical foundation for further investigations.

</details>


### [114] [How to Test Bell Nonlocality for Gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08778)
*Debarshi Das,Mir Alimuddin,Simon Storz,Yiwen Chu,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个测试引力场贝尔非定域性的实验方案,通过两个嵌入纠缠自旋的质量(如含NV中心的钻石)在光锥外产生空间叠加态,仅对引力场进行局域测量,以证明引力的量子非经典性,这是首次提出创建引力曲率EPR态、见证引力纠缠并排除局域实在论描述的完整方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于引力非经典性的测试方案尚不充分,缺乏能够(i)创建引力曲率的EPR态、(ii)见证纠缠的引力曲率、(iii)排除引力的局域实在论描述、以及(iv)实现无漏洞、完全设备无关的引力非经典性测试的最小化工具。需要一个能够超越近期提案、真正测试引力场贝尔非定域性(作为真正的非经典性标志)的实验方案。

Method: 实验方案包括以下关键步骤:(1)将两个嵌入纠缠自旋的质量(例如NV中心自旋纠缠的钻石)放置在彼此光锥之外,确保无局域性漏洞;(2)将自旋与各自质量的运动耦合,产生空间叠加态;(3)仅对两个质量的引力场进行局域测量。如果引力是量子的,两个纠缠质量将使其引力场发生纠缠,从而产生能够证明引力贝尔非定域性的关联。该方案使用处于俘获基态冷却、具有微米级空间叠加的纳米物体即可实现测试。

Result: 该实验方案能够使用俘获并基态冷却的纳米物体(具有微米级空间叠加)完成测试。若引力为量子性质,通过纠缠质量产生的引力场纠缠将导致可观测的关联,这些关联可以证明引力的贝尔非定域性。该方案首次在文献中提供了创建引力曲率EPR态、见证纠缠引力曲率、排除引力局域实在论描述,以及实现完全设备无关的无漏洞引力非经典性测试的完整工具集。

Conclusion: 本文提出的实验方案超越了近期关于引力非经典性测试的提案,首次提供了一个最小化但完整的工具集来测试引力场的贝尔非定域性。该方案能够创建引力曲率的EPR态、见证引力场纠缠、排除任何局域实在论对引力的描述,并以完全设备无关的方式实现无漏洞的引力量子非经典性测试,为验证引力的量子本质提供了关键的实验路径。

Abstract: We propose an experiment to test Bell nonlocality, a genuine nonclassicality, for the gravitational field. Two masses with embedded entangled spins (e.g., two diamonds with their NV-centre spins entangled) are placed well outside each other's light cones, ensuring a locality-loophole-free scenario. The spins are then coupled to the motion of their respective masses to generate spatial superpositions. Finally, local measurements are performed only on the gravitational fields of the two masses. If gravity is quantum, then the two entangled masses would entangle their gravitational fields, leading to correlations certifying Bell nonlocality of gravity. Trapped and ground-state cooled nano-objects with micron-sized spatial superposition are sufficient for this test. This goes beyond the recent proposals to test nonclassicality of gravity by providing, for the first time in the literature, a minimal tool to (i) create Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) state of gravitational curvatures, (ii) witness entangled gravitational curvatures, (iii) rule out any local-realist description of gravity, and (iv) achieve a loophole-free test of gravity's nonclassicality in a fully device-independent way.

</details>


### [115] [Dissipation-engineered dual-charger quantum batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08619)
*Yi-jia Yang,Yu-qiang Liu,Zheng Liu,Zhi-Hao Ma,Ming-Xing Luo,Chang-shui Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双充电器量子电池架构，通过耗散工程实现稳定的非平衡态量子储能，利用热库和冷库耦合抑制能量回流，建立具有有限功性的粒子数反转稳态，实现可扩展的持续能量存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子能量存储面临的核心挑战是在维持可提取能量的稳定非平衡态的同时抑制相干能量回流。传统的瞬态相干充电协议难以实现持续稳定的能量存储，需要开发新的策略将可逆的充电器-电池能量交换转化为持久的能量储存。

Method: 采用储库工程设计的双充电器量子电池架构：(1)热库耦合的驱动器提供激发态；(2)冷库耦合的缓存器偏置共振三体跃迁朝向充电方向；(3)抑制导致能量回流的修饰态相干性；(4)将非平衡耗散作为控制资源来稳定有用的储存能量；(5)针对均匀间隔的多能级电池系统进行理论分析和优化。

Result: 该机制成功建立了具有有限功性(ergotropy)的粒子数反转稳态，实现了可逆充电器-电池交换向持续能量存储的转化。对于均匀间隔的多能级电池：(1)储存能量和功性与可访问能级数近似呈线性标度关系；(2)储能利用率接近100%；(3)伴随的稳态热流提供了充电状态的热力学特征标志。

Conclusion: 研究证明了耗散工程作为实现稳定和可扩展量子能量存储的有效策略，超越了传统的瞬态相干充电协议。通过将耗散从传统上被视为有害的因素转变为有益的控制资源，该架构为量子电池的实用化提供了新的设计范式，展示了利用非平衡热力学过程优化量子储能系统性能的潜力。

Abstract: Suppressing coherent energy backflow while maintaining extractable energy in a stable nonequilibrium state remains a central challenge for quantum energy storage. Here, we introduce a reservoir-engineered dual-charger quantum battery architecture, in which nonequilibrium dissipation is exploited as a control resource to stabilize useful stored energy. A hot-reservoir-coupled driver supplies excitations, while a cold-reservoir-coupled cache biases the resonant three-body transition toward charging and suppresses the dressed-state coherences responsible for energy backflow. This mechanism establishes a population-inverted steady state with finite ergotropy and converts reversible charger--battery exchange into persistent energy storage. For uniformly spaced multilevel batteries, we show that the stored energy and ergotropy scale approximately linearly with the number of accessible levels, while the stored-energy utilization approaches unity. The accompanying stationary heat current provides a thermodynamic signature of the charging regime. Our results demonstrate dissipation engineering as a strategy for achieving stable and scalable quantum energy storage beyond transient coherent charging protocols.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Information Flow under String-Diagram Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09823)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Chen-Ye Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Coecke早期提出的"量子信息流"概念进行重新审视,利用弦图重写系统(特别是ZX演算)给出了明确的形式化定义,区分了依赖于图形表示的表观通路与可通过连续重写继承的真实通路,并正式定义了"Coecke流线"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coecke早期工作中提出的"量子信息流"概念缺乏明确的弦图形式化表述。为了在量子协议等物理场景中更精确地刻画信息传递路径,需要建立一个严格的数学框架来区分图形表示依赖的表观路径与本质的信息流动路径。

Method: 采用保持语义的弦图重写序列作为分析工具,区分表观通路(apparent through-paths,依赖于当前图形表示)和真实通路(genuine through paths,可通过连续重写兼容继承至终端解耦的裸线因子)。利用ZX演算(ZX弦图重写系统)作为具体例证,通过兼容继承关系正式定义"Coecke流线"。

Result: 成功建立了量子信息流的弦图形式化框架,给出了Coecke流线的严格数学定义。在ZX演算中验证了该形式化方法的有效性,展示了如何通过弦图重写追踪真实的信息流动路径。

Conclusion: 本文为量子信息流概念提供了明确的弦图理论基础。在量子协议的物理场景中,Coecke流可被解释为协议弦图内目标裸线态射因子的一种受约束的、准局域的、线状表示形式,为量子信息处理的图形化分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We revisit the notion of ``quantum information flow'' introduced in Bob Coecke's early work and seek to give it an explicit string-diagrammatic formalization. Given a semantics preserving string-diagram rewriting sequence, we first distinguish apparent through-paths, which depend on the current graphical presentation, from genuine through paths, which can be compatibly inherited through successive rewrites to a terminal decoupled bare wire factor. We then formally define a ``Coecke flow line'' in terms of this compatible inheritance relation.
  In particular, we use the ZX calculus, i.e., the ZX string diagram rewriting system, to illustrate the resulting formalism. In the physical setting of quantum protocols, a Coecke flow can be interpreted as a constrained, quasi-local, line-like presentation of a target bare wire morphism factor within the protocol string diagram.

</details>


### [117] [Born-Oppenheimer, Born-Huang, and exact factorization: quantum geometry and error in analytically transparent benchmark models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08668)
*Stephen Wiggins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文澄清了"势能面"这一术语实际上指代多个不同的物理对象(Born-Oppenheimer面、Born-Huang修正面和精确分解势能面),它们回答不同的物理问题而非竞争性定义。通过两个解析基准模型系统地比较了这些构造的性质、适用范围和几何意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子物理学中"势能面"概念存在多种不同的数学构造方式,但它们常被混淆或视为同一概念的不同近似。需要明确区分Born-Oppenheimer、Born-Huang和精确分解等不同构造的物理意义、适用条件及相互关系,特别是它们在基态和激发态上的表现差异,以及几何修正项的物理本质。

Method: 采用两个具有解析解的基准模型进行系统比较:(1)Fernández双线性耦合振子模型,获得精确分子光谱、Born-Oppenheimer谱、Born-Huang谱和基态精确分解势能面的闭式解,证明基态能量排序关系并分析激发态失效原因;(2)线性振动耦合模型,研究量子度规在避免交叉处的局域化行为。将Born-Huang对角修正项识别为质量加权量子度规,并将Fernández六阶结果重构为涉及度规和能隙加权谱矩的几何误差预算。

Result: 在双线性耦合振子模型中获得所有能量本征值和势能面的闭式表达式,严格证明了基态能量的普适排序关系(对所有允许的质量比和耦合强度成立),但该排序在激发态不均匀成立。在线性振动耦合模型中发现:量子度规在避免交叉处局域化,而总Fubini-Study长度保持为π/2,实现了电子态变化的局域化与总幅度的分离。精确分解对无节点基态是光滑的,但当激发态核边缘分布变小时条件数迅速恶化(尽管保持有限)。

Conclusion: 不同的势能面构造(Born-Oppenheimer、Born-Huang、精确分解)具有明确的物理意义和适用边界,不应被视为对同一"普适势能面"的竞争性定义。Born-Huang对角修正本质上是质量加权量子度规,反映了电子态几何性质。基态能量排序具有严格的数学保证,但激发态行为更加复杂。解析模型成功分离了近似误差、几何修正和条件数问题,为理解非绝热动力学中的不同势能面概念提供了清晰的理论框架。

Abstract: The phrase "potential energy surface" refers to several distinct objects. The Born-Oppenheimer construction gives a clamped-nucleus electronic eigenvalue, the single-surface Born-Huang construction adds the diagonal correction, and exact factorization gives a state-dependent exact scalar potential. These constructions answer different questions and should not be regarded as competing definitions of one universal surface. We compare them in two analytically transparent benchmark models. For Fernández's bilinearly coupled oscillators, the exact molecular spectrum, the Born-Oppenheimer and Born-Huang spectra, and the ground-state exact-factorization surface are obtained in closed form. We prove the ground-state ordering of these energies for every admissible mass ratio and coupling and show why it does not extend uniformly to excited states. The diagonal Born-Huang correction is identified with the mass-weighted quantum metric, and Fernández's sixth-order result is recast as a leading geometric error budget involving the metric and a gap-weighted spectral moment of the same derivative couplings. In a linear vibronic-coupling model the metric localizes at an avoided crossing while the total Fubini-Study length remains $π/2$, separating the localization of electronic-state change from its total magnitude. Exact factorization is smooth for the nodeless ground state but becomes increasingly ill-conditioned, while remaining finite, when an excited-state nuclear marginal becomes small. These models separate approximation error, geometric correction, and conditioning in a form that can be checked directly.

</details>


### [118] [Point-gap topology in amorphous non-Hermitian quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08687)
*Xue-Min Yang,Deng-Feng Li,Jian Li,Jia-Ji Zhu,Li Li,Qiu-Chi Chen,Xin-Qi Xu,Hao-Peng Zhang,Hong Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于奇异值分解的实空间拓扑不变量方法,用于识别非厄米无序系统中稳定的拓扑边缘态,克服了能谱在扰动、系统尺寸和边界条件下的不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统中存在严重问题:(1)谱缠绕数与趋肤模式的对应关系失效;(2)能谱对扰动、系统尺寸和边界条件高度敏感;(3)在无序非厄米系统中,晶格位置的不确定性使谱不稳定性更加严重,难以从本征值谱识别稳定的拓扑边缘态。需要找到更鲁棒的方法来表征拓扑性质。

Method: 提出了一种基于哈密顿量奇异值分解(SVD)的方法:(1)建立热力学极限下稳定零模奇异态与能谱mid-gap态之间的对应关系;(2)利用奇异值谱对小扰动和尺寸变化的高度鲁棒性,通过奇异值探测拓扑边缘态;(3)在实空间构造拓扑不变量来表征拓扑保护的边缘态。

Result: 奇异值谱对小扰动和系统尺寸变化具有高度鲁棒性,即使在有限尺寸系统中也能可靠地探测拓扑边缘态。基于奇异值分解构造的实空间拓扑不变量能够有效表征拓扑保护的边缘态,为在实空间探索点隙拓扑提供了通用策略。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种在实空间探索点隙拓扑的通用策略,并从新的视角重新定义了非厄米趋肤效应。通过奇异值分解方法,克服了非厄米系统(特别是无序系统)中能谱不稳定性的挑战,为识别和表征拓扑边缘态提供了鲁棒可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: Recent studies have revealed that not only does the correspondence between spectral winding numbers and skin modes break down in non-Hermitian systems, but the energy spectrum itself is highly sensitive to generic perturbations, system size, and boundary conditions. In amorphous non-Hermitian systems, where the positions of lattice sites are uncertain, the spectral instability becomes even more severe, making it difficult to identify stable topological edge states from the eigenvalue spectrum alone. To overcome this challenge, we introduce a correspondence between stable zero-mode singular states and mid-gap states of the energy spectrum in the thermodynamic limit. Because the singular value spectrum is highly robust against small perturbations and variation in size, topological edge states can be reliably probed via singular values even in finite-sized systems. Based on the singular-value decomposition of the Hamiltonian, we construct a topological invariant in real space to characterize the associated topologically protected edge states. Our approach provides a general strategy for exploring point-gap topology in real space and redefine the non-Hermitian skin effect from a new perspective.

</details>


### [119] [A Resource-Efficient Quantum Framework for Graph Coloring and Chromatic Number Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08694)
*Francesco Turro,Daniele Dragoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子编码方法用于解决图着色问题，该方法所需的量子比特数随颜色数量对数增长、随顶点数量线性增长，并通过绝热演化和新型混合哈密顿量计算色数，在约束卡车装载问题中验证了算法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题是NP难问题，在大规模图中求解极具挑战性，而许多工业优化任务都可以建模为图着色问题。现有方法在处理大规模实例时面临量子资源消耗过大的困境，需要开发更高效的量子编码方案来降低量子比特需求，使量子算法能够实际应用于工业场景。

Method: 提出了一种新的量子编码方案，其量子比特需求随颜色数对数级增长、随顶点数线性增长。采用绝热演化算法框架，设计了新型混合哈密顿量(mixer Hamiltonian)和顶点项(vertex terms)来构建演化过程。通过该量子系统的绝热演化来计算图的色数，并将算法应用于约束卡车装载问题进行验证。

Result: 成功计算了图的色数(chromatic number)，验证了所提量子编码方案的有效性。在约束卡车装载问题上的实验表明，该方法具有良好的鲁棒性，能够有效求解实际工业优化问题。量子比特的对数级缩放显著降低了量子资源需求。

Conclusion: 本文提出的量子编码方案通过对数级量子比特缩放显著提高了求解图着色问题的资源效率，结合绝热演化和新型哈密顿量设计，成功实现了色数计算并在实际工业问题中展现了鲁棒性，为量子算法解决大规模组合优化问题提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Many industrial optimization tasks can be modeled as graph coloring, where adjacent vertices must have different colors. This NP-hard problem is challenging for large graphs. We present a quantum encoding requiring qubits that scale logarithmically with the number of colors and linearly with vertices. Using adiabatic evolution with a novel mixer Hamiltonian and vertex terms, we compute the chromatic number and demonstrate robustness by solving constrained truck loading problems.

</details>


### [120] [DA-CASE: reusable measurements for adaptive quantum subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08739)
*Ginanjar Utama,Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了二进制自适应Clifford代数子空间本征求解器(DA-CASE),这是一种量子子空间方法,通过单个参考态生成基态并从缓存的泡利期望值重建矩阵,将多个制备态的成本转换为更宽的测量集,并通过对易层级、协方差感知分配和正则化技术优化测量资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子子空间方法通常仅通过基维度或能量误差进行比较,但实际实验成本主要来自不同的态制备、测量设置和采样分配。现有方法缺乏对这些实际资源成本的系统性权衡和精确核算,需要一种能够明确量化制备态数量与测量复杂度之间权衡关系的新架构,同时建立包含上下文、测量设置、采样数、电路深度和后选择重试等要素的完整资源账本。

Method: DA-CASE方法采用单参考态架构:1)从一个参考态|ψ⟩通过Clifford代数生成器A_i构造虚拟方向态A_i|ψ⟩作为基态;2)从该参考态上缓存的一组泡利期望值重建重叠矩阵、哈密顿量矩阵和可观测量矩阵;3)引入参考条件对称性测试来认证子空间跨度;4)采用二进制对易层级将测量设置从913个qubit级对易设置压缩到64个完全对易设置;5)应用协方差感知采样分配策略;6)使用模式级重叠正则化处理病态矩阵问题。

Result: 在冻结八量子比特H₄哈密顿量测试中:两种生成器分辨率达到相同的九维子空间和机器精度的相同能量,保留的测量集从7371个泡利词减少到2240个;二进制对易层级将行列式测量集从913个qubit级对易设置减少到64个完全对易设置;在四量子比特有限采样诊断中,协方差感知分配将投影矩阵方差目标降低68.9%;模式级重叠正则化消除了灾难性能量估计并降低RMSE,但使中位误差相对于固定截断增加一倍。

Conclusion: DA-CASE提供了一种单参考态测量架构,通过将多个态制备成本转换为测量集复杂度来实现资源权衡。该方法建立了完整的资源账本框架,将上下文、测量设置、采样数、电路深度和后选择重试等要素置于适当的单位体系中。研究基于小规模精确和蒙特卡洛结果,不是硬件演示、规模化结果或量子优势声明,而是为量子子空间方法的实际资源成本评估提供了系统性工具和分析框架。

Abstract: Quantum subspace methods are often compared by basis dimension or energyerror, although their dominant experimental costs arise from different statepreparations, measurement settings, and shot allocations. We present theDyadic Adaptive Clifford-Algebra Subspace Eigensolver (DA-CASE), whose basisstates are virtual directions $A_i|ψ\rangle$ generated from one reference.Overlap, Hamiltonian, and observable matrices are reconstructed from onecached set of Pauli expectations on that reference. The method thereforetrades multiple prepared basis states for a potentially wide measurement bank.We make that trade explicit on a frozen eight-qubit H$_4$ Hamiltonian. Twogenerator resolutions reach the same nine-dimensional subspace and the sameenergy to machine precision, while the retained bank changes from 7371 to 2240Pauli words. A reference-conditioned symmetry test certifies the narrower spanwithout asserting that its individual Pauli words conserve the sector asabstract operators. Independently, a dyadic commuting hierarchy reduces thedeterminant bank from 913 qubit-wise-commuting settings to 64 fully commutingsettings, while exposing the added logical-CX cost. In a separate four-qubitfinite-shot diagnostic, covariance-aware allocation reduces theprojected-matrix variance target by 68.9%. Mode-wise overlap regularizationremoves the observed catastrophic energy estimates and lowers RMSE, butdoubles the median error relative to a fixed cutoff. These are small-instanceexact and Monte Carlo results, not a hardware demonstration, scaling result,or quantum advantage claim. The contribution is a single-referencemeasurement architecture and a resource ledger that keeps contexts, settings,shots, circuit depth, and post-selection retries in their proper units.

</details>


### [121] [Promise and Challenges of Distimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08745)
*Joshua Carlo A. Casapao,Ananda G. Maity,Naphan Benchasattabuse,Michal Hajdušek,Akihito Soeda,Rodney Van Meter,David Elkouss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为"distimation"的新概念,它将量子网络中传统的纠缠态质量估计和纠缠提纯两个独立的链路层协议合并为一个,通过直接从纠缠提纯过程中产生的经典校正子中提取状态估计数据,从而减少原始纠缠对的消耗、降低延迟并简化网络控制平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子网络中,传统方法需要两个独立的链路层协议分别进行纠缠态质量估计和纠缠提纯,每个协议都要消耗宝贵的纠缠对资源。这种"先测试后提纯"的方式在纠缠生成仍是严重瓶颈的近期硬件平台上效率低下。因此需要一种更高效的方法来减少资源消耗、降低延迟并简化网络操作。

Method: Distimation方法将质量估计和纠缠提纯两个协议合并,核心思路是直接从纠缠提纯过程中生成的经典校正子(syndrome)中提取状态估计数据,从而取消专门的"测试-提纯"分离阶段。文章调研了distimation的核心原理,量化了其在实际架构中的预期收益,并提出了相关的研究路线图,包括精确的本地设备建模和追踪时变源的协议等工程挑战。

Result: Distimation方法能够显著降低量子网络运行所需的原始纠缠对数量,减少操作延迟,并简化网络控制平面。该方法对于纠缠生成仍是严重瓶颈的近期硬件平台特别具有吸引力。文章量化了该方法在实际架构中的预期性能增益。

Conclusion: Distimation作为一种创新范式,通过整合质量估计和纠缠提纯过程,为量子网络提供了更高效的资源利用方案。尽管引入了精确本地设备建模和时变源追踪等新的工程挑战,但其在减少资源消耗和降低延迟方面的优势使其有望成为未来量子网络的标准构建模块。文章为实现这一目标提出了明确的研究路线图。

Abstract: Estimating the quality of raw entangled states and distilling high-fidelity entanglement traditionally require two separate link-layer protocols in a quantum network stack, each consuming its own share of fragile entangled pairs. The recently introduced distimation concept merges these two protocols by directly extracting state estimation data from the classical syndromes generated during entanglement distillation. By removing the dedicated "test-then-distill'' stage, distimation lowers the number of raw entangled pairs required to operate the network, reduces latency, and streamlines the network control plane. The paradigm is especially attractive for near-term hardware platforms, where entanglement generation remains a severe bottleneck. Alongside these promises, distimation introduces new engineering challenges, ranging from accurate local-device modeling to protocols for tracking time-varying sources. This article surveys the core principles of distimation, quantifies expected gains for realistic architectures, and outlines a research roadmap toward making distimation a standard building block in future quantum networks.

</details>


### [122] [Symmetry Constraints Regularize Neural Quantum State Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08798)
*Turbasu Chatterjee,Manas Sajjan,Songbo Xie,Elliott Love,Vinit Singh,Bojko N. Bakalov,Sabre Kais*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将哈密顿对称性直接嵌入神经量子态(NQS)的变分参数化中，通过对称性编译技术大幅减少冗余参数，显著改善优化几何结构，在保持基态精度的同时实现参数压缩和训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态虽然具有强大的表达能力，但其优化过程常受到冗余参数和病态优化landscape的困扰。现有方法未能充分利用物理系统固有的哈密顿对称性来简化参数空间和改善优化几何结构，导致训练成本高、收敛困难。

Method: 针对Boltzmann族神经量子态，通过沿物理几何轨道绑定局部Pauli-Z生成器来强制对称性约束，在优化前解析地压缩可训练参数空间。引入基于雅可比矩阵和Hessian矩阵的几何度量框架，量化优化landscape中对应高质量低能解的物理可达状态空间占比。该方法在横场伊辛模型(TFIM)和XXZ自旋链上进行验证。

Result: 对称性编译在保持基态精度的前提下消除了绝大多数冗余参数。在大规模TFIM系统中，强空间约束将数千个参数压缩至数十个，实现显著的运行时加速。几何诊断表明，对称性产生了更有利的目标感知几何结构，将可达状态空间集中在低能解附近，同时保留宽广的目标吸引盆。

Conclusion: 对称性编译将神经量子态的表达能力集中于与目标问题相关的状态上,在不牺牲精度的情况下减少模型规模和训练成本。该方法通过几何正则化优化问题,为高效训练神经量子态提供了一条原理性路径,证明了将物理对称性嵌入变分参数化的有效性。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) offer highly expressive variational wavefunctions, but their optimization is frequently bottlenecked by redundant parameters and poorly conditioned landscapes. We demonstrate that embedding Hamiltonian symmetries directly into the variational parameterization geometrically regularizes this learning problem. For Boltzmann-family NQS, we enforce symmetries by tying local Pauli-$Z$ generators along physical geometric orbits, analytically collapsing the trainable coefficient space prior to optimization. To quantify the resulting optimization geometry, we introduce a geometric metric built on the Jacobian and Hessian of the optimization landscape. This framework evaluates the fraction of the physically accessible state space that corresponds to high-quality, low-energy solutions. Evaluating our approach on transverse-field Ising (TFIM) and XXZ spin chains shows that symmetry compilation excises the vast majority of parameters while maintaining ground-state accuracy within the resolution of the reported benchmarks. In large TFIM systems, strong spatial constraints compress thousands of parameters down to tens, delivering substantial runtime accelerations. Our geometric diagnostics indicate that symmetry produces a more favorable target-aware geometry by concentrating the reachable state space around low-energy solutions while retaining broad target basins. Together, our results indicate that symmetry compilation concentrates the expressive power of NQS on states relevant to the target problem, thereby reducing model size and training cost without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [State preparation via measurement and feedback: pushing relations, state structures, and non-invertible symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08821)
*Yabo Li,Aditi Mitra,Tsung-Cheng Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化方案，通过测量与反馈(MF)量子线路在常数深度内制备一维长程纠缠态。该方案基于可推动缺陷的概念，利用矩阵乘积态的虚键算符及其推动关系对有限深度MF可制备态进行分类，并构造相应的制备线路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然带测量和酉反馈的量子线路能在常数深度内制备长程纠缠态，但针对给定目标态系统化构造MF制备线路的方法仍未得到充分研究。需要建立一套完整的理论框架来刻画哪些态可以通过有限深度MF线路制备，以及如何具体构造这些线路。

Method: 提出基于"可推动缺陷"的系统化构造方案：1) 定义可推动缺陷为矩阵乘积态的虚键算符，可通过物理反馈酉算符在张量网络中推动；2) 利用可推动缺陷集合及其推动关系对有限深度MF可制备态进行分类；3) 对目标态|A⟩关联一个辅助态|B⟩，使得|A⟩可通过单轮MF线路从|B⟩制备；4) 迭代该过程直到关联态可由有限深度局域酉(FDLU)线路制备；5) 对于开边界矩阵乘积态，该方案在左条件反馈修正下是完备的。

Result: 1) 建立了可推动缺陷及推动关系作为通过测量和反馈制备量子态的统一原理；2) 证明当|B⟩可由FDLU线路制备时，|A⟩可通过单轮MF线路从乘积态制备；3) 对开边界矩阵乘积态给出完备的制备方案；4) 揭示了MF线路与非可逆对称性之间的内在联系：具有特定推动关系类别的态可通过Tambara-Yamagami对偶算符或连续余弦对称算符(融合规则为L_α L_{α'} = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'})及其推广与乘积态相关联。

Conclusion: 可推动缺陷及其推动关系提供了一个系统化框架，完整刻画了一维量子态的有限深度测量反馈可制备性，并建立了与非可逆对称性理论的深刻联系。该方案不仅解决了MF制备线路的系统化构造问题，还揭示了量子态制备、张量网络结构与对称性之间的统一数学结构。

Abstract: Quantum circuits with measurements and unitary feedback (MF) can prepare long-range entangled states in constant depth, but a systematic construction of the MF preparation circuit for a given target state remains underexplored. We develop such a scheme for one-dimensional states, based on the notion of pushable defects: virtual-bond operators of a matrix product state that can be pushed through the tensor at the price of a physical feedback unitary. We show that the set of pushable defects, together with their pushing relations classifies finite-depth MF-preparable states and dictates their preparation circuits. To each class of the target state $|A\rangle$, we associate a state $|B\rangle$ from which $|A\rangle$ can be prepared using a 1-round MF circuit; in particular, $|A\rangle$ is preparable from a product state using a circuit with 1 round of MF whenever $|B\rangle$ is preparable by a finite-depth local unitary (FDLU) circuit. For a general target state, the scheme is obtained by iterating this procedure until the associated state is FDLU-preparable. For open-boundary matrix product states, the scheme is complete: it constructs a preparation circuit whenever finite-depth MF preparation with left-conditioned feedback corrections is possible. Pushable defects and pushing relations thus emerge as a unifying principle for quantum state preparation via measurements and feedback. This characterization further reveals an intrinsic connection between MF circuits and non-invertible symmetries: states with certain classes of pushing relations are related to a product state by Tambara-Yamagami duality operators, or by continuous cosine symmetry operators with fusion rules $L_α L_{α'} = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'}$, together with their generalizations up to (not necessarily transversal) gates.

</details>


### [124] [Two-copy distillability of one-copy-undistillable negative-partial-transpose states in every dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08836)
*Gelo Noel M. Tabia,Kai-Siang Chen,Min-Hsiu Hsieh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子纠缠理论中的NPT态可蒸馏性问题,证明了DiVincenzo标准族中某个单拷贝不可蒸馏态在任意局部维度d≥3时已是双拷贝可蒸馏的,从而推翻了该族中所有单拷贝不可蒸馏区域在任意多拷贝下仍不可蒸馏的猜想,并在同一对称约化族中严格证明了存在双拷贝行为相反的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠理论中的一个基本未解问题是:单拷贝不可蒸馏的NPT态在有限多拷贝情况下是否变得可蒸馏。DiVincenzo等人引入的双参数标准族作为该问题的对称约化测试平台,存在关于其单拷贝不可蒸馏区域在任意多拷贝下仍保持不可蒸馏的猜想,需要通过严格证明来验证或推翻这一猜想。

Method: 采用均匀等范数紧框架(uniform equal-norm tight-frame)构造方法,在每个维度下给出显式的Schmidt秩2证明;利用双拷贝和三拷贝见证算子(witness)来认证可蒸馏性;结合Werner态的最新结果和DiVincenzo等人的传播论证(propagation argument)来确定不可蒸馏区域;通过对称约化族中的参数空间分析来建立可蒸馏区域的内边界。

Result: 在任意局部维度d≥3的情况下,证明了标准族中一个特定的单拷贝不可蒸馏态已经是双拷贝可蒸馏的;双拷贝见证算子不仅认证了该反例,还认证了其周围的开邻域为双拷贝可蒸馏区域;独立构造的三拷贝见证算子扩大了参数空间中可蒸馏区域的内边界;同时确定了相邻的单拷贝不可蒸馏但双拷贝仍不可蒸馏的区域;在单个对称约化族中严格认证了双拷贝行为相反的态,两者之间存在有限拷贝可蒸馏性尚未确定的大量区域。

Conclusion: 成功推翻了DiVincenzo标准族中整个单拷贝不可蒸馏区域在任意多拷贝下仍不可蒸馏的猜想,证明了至少存在从单拷贝不可蒸馏到双拷贝可蒸馏的转变。研究揭示了单个对称约化族内部存在复杂的有限拷贝蒸馏行为:既有双拷贝可蒸馏态,也有双拷贝不可蒸馏态,二者之间被大量有限拷贝可蒸馏性未定的区域分隔。这一结果为理解NPT态的有限拷贝蒸馏性质提供了重要的反例和结构性认识。

Abstract: Whether negative-partial-transpose (NPT) states that are undistillable from one copy become distillable from finitely many copies remains a basic open problem in entanglement theory. We study the canonical two-parameter family of DiVincenzo \textit{et al.}, introduced as a symmetry-reduced testbed for this question. We prove that a distinguished one-copy-undistillable state in this family is already two-copy distillable in every local dimension $d\geq 3$. A uniform equal-norm tight-frame construction gives explicit Schmidt-rank-two certificates in every dimension, thereby disproving the conjecture that the entire one-copy-undistillable region of the canonical family remains undistillable for arbitrarily many copies. The same witnesses certify an open two-copy-distillable neighborhood around the counterexample, while separately constructed three-copy witnesses enlarge the inner bounds for the distillable region in the surrounding parameter space. In contrast, recent results for Werner states, together with the propagation argument of DiVincenzo \textit{et al.}, establish a neighboring region of one-copy-undistillable states that remains two-copy undistillable. Thus a single symmetry-reduced family contains rigorously certified states with opposite two-copy behavior, separated by a substantial region whose finite-copy distillability remains unresolved.

</details>


### [125] [The Logic of Partitions and Partition Logics: Ore's Correspondence, Contextual Pasting, and Direct-Sum Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08931)
*Karl Svozil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了"分割逻辑"的两层构造：自动机模型中选定分割生成的布尔事件代数及其上下文粘合结构，以及Ellerman框架中分割作为分类的细化与运算。文章通过Ore对应关系连接有限集分割与布尔代数，分析了交、并运算的代数性质，并将直和分解作为向量空间类比，应用于Gleason和Kochen-Specker定理中的全局一致性赋值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，分割逻辑在自动机事件代数与Ellerman分类框架中存在两个层次的构造，但Ore对应关系未能将粘合载体与分割集合Part(U)统一识别，也未将粘合操作视为分割运算。研究者需要理解分割生成的布尔代数之间的交、并关系，区分共享事件、原子交织与继承的具体序关系，并建立与向量空间直和分解的类比，以支持量子逻辑中全局上下文一致性赋值的应用需求。

Method: 文章采用Ore对应关系将有限集U的每个分割生成元π映射到其布尔代数BA(π)，建立分割运算与代数运算的联系：交运算π∧σ对应布尔代数的交集BA(π)∩BA(σ)，并运算π∨σ对应布尔代数并集的布尔代数生成⟨BA(π)∪BA(σ)⟩_BA。通过中式灯笼、萤火虫和三角形等具体实例区分共享事件、原子交织和继承的具体序。引入Ellerman的直和分解(DSD)作为向量空间类比：正交DSD的分量投影分解恒等算子并编码互斥结果，但分量不是向量的等价类。最后将该框架应用于Gleason定理和Kochen-Specker定理，要求投影上的全局上下文一致性赋值。

Result: 建立了分割运算与布尔代数运算的精确对应关系：交运算捕获公共事件代数，并运算给出外围布尔闭包。通过中式灯笼、萤火虫和三角形实例成功区分了三种不同的结构特征（共享事件、原子交织、继承具体序）。证明了直和分解的分量投影能够分解恒等算子并编码互斥结果，尽管分量本身不构成向量的等价类。该理论框架可应用于Gleason定理和Kochen-Specker定理中，支持在投影上构造全局上下文一致性赋值。

Conclusion: 分割逻辑通过Ore对应关系在自动机事件代数与Ellerman分类框架之间建立了系统联系，交、并运算分别对应公共事件代数与布尔闭包。直和分解为分割逻辑提供了向量空间类比，其投影结构支持量子逻辑中的全局一致性赋值。该理论框架统一了不同层次的分割构造，为量子测量理论中的上下文性问题提供了数学基础，具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: The term ``partition logic'' denotes two constructions at different levels. In automaton and generalized-urn models, selected partitions generate Boolean event algebras whose contextwise union forms a concrete pasted event structure; in Ellerman's framework, whole partitions are classifications governed by refinement and partition operations. For a finite set $U$, Ore's correspondence maps each generator $π$ to its Boolean algebra $\BA(π)$, but it neither identifies the pasted carrier with $\Part(U)$ nor makes pasting a partition operation. It yields $\BA(π\wedgeσ)=\BA(π)\cap\BA(σ)$ and $\BA(π\veeσ)=\langle\BA(π)\cup\BA(σ)\rangle_{\rm BA}$, where $\langle\cdot\rangle_{\rm BA}$ denotes Boolean-algebra generation. Thus meet captures the common event algebra, whereas join gives the ambient Boolean closure. Chinese-lantern, Firefly, and triangular examples distinguish shared events, atomic intertwining, and inherited concrete order. Ellerman's direct-sum decompositions (DSDs) provide a vector-space analogue: component projections of an orthogonal DSD resolve the identity and encode exclusive outcomes, but its components are not equivalence classes of vectors. Gleason and Kochen--Specker applications require globally context-consistent valuations on those projections.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Gouy phase singularities in a propagating biphoton state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08962)
*Takumi Jinushi,Hirokazu Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了纠缠光子对在纵向传播空间中由量子Gouy相位产生的非局域相位奇点(NPSs),证明了这些拓扑缺陷仅存在于联合波函数中,并揭示了其拓扑荷、传播位置与径向模式结构的关系,将非局域奇异光学从横向空间扩展到纵向传播动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位奇点是拓扑光学的重要研究对象,已知可在纠缠多光子态的联合波函数中非局域地出现。然而,现有研究主要关注横向空间关联,尚未探索纠缠光子对在纵向传播过程中的非局域相位奇点现象。本文旨在研究量子Gouy相位如何在二维纵向传播空间中产生非局域相位奇点,并建立传播距离作为纠缠光子对拓扑结构的坐标空间。

Method: 研究采用Laguerre-Gaussian光束泵浦的自发参量下转换(SPDC)过程产生纠缠光子对,信号光子和闲频光子在不同纵向距离上独立传播。通过分析径向模式依赖的Gouy相位累积效应,计算双光子空间模式分量之间的相干干涉,识别联合波函数中出现强度零点和量子化相位缠绕的条件。针对泵浦径向模式数为p的情况,推导非局域相位奇点的拓扑荷大小、传播位置及符号与泵浦和相位匹配函数的瑞利范围的关系,并分析形成这些奇点所需的径向模式结构。

Result: 研究发现量子Gouy相位在纠缠光子对的纵向传播空间中产生孤立的非局域相位奇点,其拓扑荷大小为泵浦径向模式数p。奇点的传播位置和拓扑荷符号由泵浦和相位匹配函数的瑞利范围决定。关键发现是可分离的双光子态无法支持孤立的纵向非局域相位奇点,表明这种现象本质上需要量子纠缠。实验证实了径向模式依赖的Gouy相位累积导致双光子空间模式分量之间的相消干涉,在特定传播距离组合处产生强度零点和量子化相位缠绕。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将非局域奇异光学从横向空间关联拓展到纵向传播动力学,确立了传播距离可以作为纠缠光子对拓扑结构的坐标空间。量子Gouy相位在纵向传播中产生的非局域相位奇点是量子纠缠的本质体现,可分离态无法支持此类拓扑结构。这些发现为理解纠缠光子系统的拓扑性质提供了新视角,可能在量子信息处理、量子成像和高维量子态操控等领域具有应用潜力,为基于传播距离的量子拓扑光学开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Phase singularities are topological defects around which the phase winds by an integer multiple of $2π$. In entangled multiphoton states, they can emerge nonlocally in the joint wavefunction rather than in the field of either subsystem alone. Here we show that the quantum Gouy phase generates nonlocal phase singularities (NPSs) in the two-dimensional longitudinal propagation space of entangled photon pairs. We consider photon pairs produced via spontaneous parametric down-conversion pumped by a Laguerre--Gaussian beam, with the signal and idler photons propagating independently over different longitudinal distances. The accumulated radial-mode-dependent Gouy phases induce destructive interference among biphoton spatial-mode components, producing isolated intensity nulls with quantized phase winding. For a pump with radial mode number $p$, the NPS topological charges have magnitude $p$, whereas their propagation positions and charge signs are governed by the Rayleigh ranges of the pump and phase-matching functions. We further identify the radial-mode structure required for their formation, showing that separable biphoton states cannot support isolated longitudinal NPSs. Our results extend nonlocal singular optics from transverse spatial correlations to longitudinal propagation dynamics and establish propagation distance as a coordinate space for topological structures in entangled photon pairs.

</details>


### [127] [Multi-agent discovery of practical quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08996)
*Dongheng Qian,Tianyi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个多智能体框架,用于发现实用的量子低密度奇偶校验(qLDPC)码。该框架结合了专家提议与审查、持久科学记忆、可执行程序的长期演化等机制,在满足实际约束条件下,成功发现了多个在码率-距离性能上领先或具有竞争力的有限长度qLDPC码实例,为量子纠错提供了硬件相关的候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找有限长度的实用qLDPC码是一个具有挑战性的设计问题,需要在优化码性能的同时满足实际约束条件。受人工智能在科学发现领域最新进展的启发,研究者希望开发一种自动化搜索框架,以系统地探索和发现满足实际硬件需求的高性能qLDPC码构造。传统的手工设计方法效率低下,难以穷尽复杂的构造空间,因此需要智能化的搜索策略。

Method: 研究开发了一个多智能体框架,用于在闭环搜索中发现qLDPC码。该框架的核心要素包括:(1)专家提议与审查机制;(2)持久科学记忆;(3)可执行程序的长期演化;(4)确定性构造与评估。搜索空间聚焦于coset-orbit平衡积码,涵盖了bicycle码和lifted-product构造以及非正规子群作用。为满足实际约束,搜索限制在二元CSS码,块长度n≤400,总权重w≤10的范围内。代码通过BP-OSD解码协议在码容量去极化噪声下进行评估。

Result: 在所考虑的每个权重类别中,框架都发现了在码率-距离性能上领先或具有竞争力的码。代表性实例包括:w=7时的[[288,16,18]]码、w=9时的[[288,18,18]]码、w=10时的[[234,28,18]]码。搜索还发现了结构独特的高性能构造,如[[336,12,≤24]]候选码和[[368,18,16]]码,这两者都是具有非正规子群作用的真正平衡积构造。在BP-OSD解码协议下进行码容量去极化噪声评估时,所发现的码也表现出低逻辑失败率。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了与硬件相关的有限长度qLDPC码候选方案,可用于进一步的实验评估,展示了结构化智能体搜索如何为科学发现做出贡献。多智能体框架成功地在满足实际约束的前提下,系统地发现了多个高性能的qLDPC码构造,包括一些结构新颖的非正规子群平衡积码,证明了AI驱动的自动化搜索在量子纠错码设计中的有效性和潜力。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes can encode multiple logical qubits using sparse parity checks, yet searching for useful finite-length instances remains a challenging design problem because code performance must be optimized while satisfying practical constraints. Motivated by recent advances in artificial-intelligence agents for scientific discovery, we develop a multi-agent framework for discovering practical qLDPC codes. The framework combines specialist proposal and review, persistent scientific memory, long-horizon evolution of executable programs, and deterministic construction and evaluation within a closed-loop search. These programs instantiate coset-orbit balanced-product codes, providing a search space that includes bicycle and lifted-product constructions as well as non-normal subgroup actions. To incorporate practical constraints, we restrict the search to binary CSS codes with block length $n\leq400$ and overall weight $w\leq10$. Within this regime, the framework discovers codes with leading or competitive rate--distance performance in every weight class considered, with representative instances including $[[288,16,18]]$ at $w=7$, $[[288,18,18]]$ at $w=9$, and $[[234,28,18]]$ at $w=10$. The search also uncovers structurally distinct, high-performing constructions, including a $[[336,12,\leq24]]$ candidate and a $[[368,18,16]]$ code, both of which are genuine balanced-product constructions with non-normal subgroup actions. When evaluated under code-capacity depolarizing noise using a common BP-OSD decoding protocol, the discovered codes also exhibit low logical failure rates. Together, these results provide hardware-relevant finite-length candidates for further experimental evaluation and show how structured agentic search can contribute to scientific discovery.

</details>


### [128] [Universal entropic occupation statistics in disordered bosonic resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09007)
*Orest Bucicovschi,David A. Meyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究可编程微腔中的光子气体热力学，发现玻色子态计数产生"熵阶梯"现象，揭示了活跃腔占据分数与温度的渐近线性关系，并在双模情况下发现条件分布遵循Gauss-Kuzmin分布，建立了光子热力学与度量数论的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程微腔系统支持巨正则光子气体，但其玻色统计特性和热力学行为尚未被充分理解。特别是在化学势阈值附近，失谐密度分布如何影响光子占据统计，以及这种统计与数学理论（如度量数论）之间可能存在的深层联系，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用玻色子态计数方法分析微腔光子气体的统计力学性质。针对化学势阈值处失谐密度连续且非零的情况，研究低温极限下活跃腔占据分数的渐近行为和条件概率分布的普适极限。特别分析了均匀无序情况，并详细研究了双模系统的条件分布规律，将其与Gauss-Kuzmin分布建立联系。

Result: 发现玻色子态计数产生"熵阶梯"结构，导致最可几总占据数呈现阶梯状分布。当失谐密度在化学势阈值处连续且非零时，活跃腔占据分数在低温下呈渐近线性关系，条件概率分布存在普适极限。对于均匀无序情况,该分布律在有限温度区间内精确成立。双模系统的条件分布恰好是Gauss-Kuzmin分布，建立了光子热力学与度量数论之间的桥梁。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了可编程微腔光子气体的独特统计力学特性，玻色子态计数导致的熵阶梯现象及其引起的普适统计行为，为理解光子热力学提供了新视角。特别是双模情况下与Gauss-Kuzmin分布的联系，建立了光子物理与数论之间意想不到的跨学科联系。研究提出了有限阵列和染料微腔两种实验验证方案，为后续实验研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Programmable microcavities support grand-canonical photon gases. We show that bosonic state counting creates an entropic staircase of most-probable total occupations. For detuning density continuous and nonzero at the chemical-potential threshold, the active-cell fraction is asymptotically linear at low temperature and the conditional law has a universal limit; for uniform disorder the law is exact over a finite temperature interval. For two modes it is the Gauss--Kuzmin distribution, linking photonic thermodynamics and metric number theory. We outline finite-array and dye-microcavity tests.

</details>


### [129] [The Magic Scroll: Leveraging biased noise to improve magic state cultivation in register-based architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09018)
*Ian D. Thorvaldson,Jeffrey Marshall,Jack R. Craig,Samuel K. Gorman,Charles D. Hill,Michelle Y. Simmons*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了在双量子比特寄存器架构下实现6.6.6和4.8.8色码以及双层和折叠表面码的高效方法,并通过结合色码和双层码开发了"Magic Scroll"技术,将魔态培育体积改进3倍,同时利用噪声偏置和高量子比特连接性显著提升容错量子计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多个量子计算平台正在探索超越最近邻平面晶格的高连接性量子比特架构,理论研究也在考虑如何利用这种高连接性(如非局域LDPC码)和噪声偏置(比特翻转和相位翻转错误概率不等)来改进容错码。然而,如何在实际的寄存器架构中有效实现这些先进编码方案,并结合噪声偏置特性来提升容错量子计算的性能和资源效率,仍是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 1. 提出使用双量子比特寄存器高效实现6.6.6和4.8.8色码以及双层和折叠表面码的方法;2. 利用噪声偏置特性避免钩子错误(hook errors),使性能与表面码相当;3. 开发"Magic Scroll"技术,结合色码和双层码来改进魔态培育过程;4. 将该技术进一步扩展到魔态蒸馏过程以提升蒸馏性能;5. 系统性地展示如何利用噪声偏置和高量子比特连接性来降低错误率和减少量子体积。

Result: Magic Scroll技术将魔态培育体积改进了3倍,支持魔态|T⟩保真度低至10^-9;在魔态蒸馏方面,对于错误率约10^-15的情况,蒸馏体积改进了3倍;所提出的色码和双层码实现方案在利用噪声偏置后达到了与表面码相当的性能;证明了寄存器架构能够有效利用高连接性和噪声偏置来提升容错量子计算效率。

Conclusion: 本研究通过系统性地结合双量子比特寄存器架构、噪声偏置特性和高量子比特连接性,成功开发了改进的色码和表面码实现方案以及创新的Magic Scroll技术。这些技术显著降低了容错量子计算所需的量子资源体积(3倍改进)并提升了错误率性能,为基于寄存器的量子计算架构提供了实用的容错方案,展示了如何在实际量子硬件约束下优化容错量子计算的路径。

Abstract: Multiple quantum computing platforms across neutral atoms [1], nitrogen vacancy centres [2, 3], gate-defined dots [4-6] and 14|15 phosphorus atom qubits [7, 8] in silicon are experimentally exploring the use of high connectivity qubits, beyond that of nearest-neighbour planar lattices. Theoretical works consider modifications to fault-tolerant codes to leverage this higher qubit connectivity, such as non-local LDPC codes [9], inspiring superconducting [10] and photonic [11] platforms to also seek non-planar connectivity. In addition, separate theoretical works consider biased noise, where bit- and phase-flip errors are not equally likely. In this work, we present efficient methods for implementing 6.6.6 and 4.8.8 colour codes, as well as bilayer and folded surface codes, using two-qubit registers. We also leverage noise bias to avoid hook errors, demonstrating comparable performance to the surface code. By combining colour and bilayer codes, we show how magic state cultivation procedures [12-14] can be improved, in a procedure we refer to as the Magic Scroll. Not only does the Magic Scroll escape to a standard surface code, it also improves cultivation volumes by 3x and supports magic $|T\rangle$ state fidelities as low as $10^{-9}$. We show that this technique can be further leveraged to improve distillation performance, showing a 3x improvement to distillation volumes for error rates of $\sim 10^{-15}$. Through these constructions, we demonstrate techniques to leverage noise bias and high qubit connectivity, showing how register-based architectures can improve error rates and reduce quantum volumes in fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [130] [Knot your average qutrit: Measurement-induced entanglement splitting and the cabling dictionary for GHZ and W States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09076)
*Sougata Bhattacharyya,Sovik Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三能级量子系统(qutrit)中多体纠缠态在单粒子测量后的纠缠分裂行为,发现传统的GHZ类和W类分类在测量诱导的纠缠分裂问题上并不适用。真正的分类标准是态的指标结构:重复指标(如|W_{p,p,q}>)与全不同指标(如|W_{0,1,2}>和|GHZ_3>)的差异,并通过扩展Aravind的拓扑关联模型提出了"双股缠绕"图像来描述这种分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上多体纠缠态按GHZ类和W类分类,预期它们在测量下表现不同。然而对于qutrit系统,尚不清楚这种分类是否真正反映了测量诱导纠缠分裂的行为差异。需要系统研究qutrit态在单粒子测量后的纠缠分裂规律,并找到更本质的分类标准。同时需要将Aravind用于qubit的拓扑关联模型扩展到qutrit系统,以直观理解更复杂的Schmidt秩结构。

Method: 1) 将Aravind对应关系(将纠缠建模为拓扑链接,投影测量建模为物理切割)从qubit扩展到qutrit系统;2) 完整推导GHZ型qutrit态(|GHZ_3>)和对称W类qutrit态家族(包括六个"两同一异"态|W_{p,p,q}^{sym}>和一个"全不同"态|W_{0,1,2}>)在计算基(CB)和互不偏基(MUBs)下的测量诱导纠缠分裂;3) 计算每种情况下所有测量结果的精确本征值和Schmidt秩;4) 提出"双股缠绕"扩展来表示qutrit的三值Schmidt秩(R∈{1,2,3}),区别于qubit的二值情况(R∈{1,2})。

Result: 研究发现:|W_{0,1,2}>态与|GHZ_3>态表现相似,在每个测量基下都显示单一的、与测量结果无关的残余Schmidt秩;而|W_{p,p,q}^{sym}>态则表现出概率加权的、依赖于测量结果的行为。这表明真正的结构分界线是"重复指标versus全不同指标"的包结构,而非传统的"GHZ类versus W类"。通过"双股缠绕"图像成功扩展了Aravind的"环-链"模型,可以描述qutrit的三值Schmidt秩结构。

Conclusion: 对于qutrit系统的测量诱导纠缠分裂问题,传统的GHZ类与W类分类并非本质分类标准。真正重要的结构特征是量子态指标的重复性:全不同指标态(|W_{0,1,2}>和|GHZ_3>)表现出与结果无关的确定性分裂模式,而重复指标态(|W_{p,p,q}^{sym}>)则呈现结果依赖的概率性行为。提出的"双股缠绕"拓扑图像为理解qutrit纠缠结构提供了直观工具,但作者明确指出这是一种标记约定而非严格的拓扑不变量,未来需要进一步工作将其发展为真正的拓扑理论。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement is conventionally classified by state families viz. family of GHZ and W class of states, with each family expected to behave differently under measurement. We show that, at least for the question of how entanglement splits after a single-particle measurement, this is not the division that matters for qutrits. Extending the Aravind's correspondence (which models entanglement as topological linking, and projective measurement as physically cutting a ring from an interlinked configuration\cite{aravind1997}) from qubits to qutrits, we derive the complete measurement-induced entanglement splitting of the GHZ type qutrit state i.e |GHZ_3> and of the full family of symmetric W class qutrit states, six two same - one different states i.e |{W_{p,p,q}^{sym}}> and one all - different state i.e. |W_{0,1,2}>, under both the computational basis (CB) and the mutually unbiased bases (MUBs), obtaining exact eigenvalues and Schmidt ranks for every outcome in every case. We see that the |W_{0,1,2}> state behaves similarly as |GHZ_3> state, a single, outcome-independent residual rank in each basis, while the |{W_{p,p,q}^{sym}> states alone show probability-weighted, outcome-dependent behaviour. The relevant structural line is therefore repeated-index versus all-different-index bag structure, not GHZ class versus $W$ class. We express this classification using a \textit{two-strand cabling} extension of Aravind's \textit{ring-and-link} picture. This is needed because the qutrit residual Schmidt rank (R) takes three values, R belonging to {1,2,3}, rather than the qubit binary (i.e. R belonging to {1,2}). We are explicit throughout that this cabling dictionary is a labeling convention built to reproduce an independently computed Schmidt rank, not a topological invariant derived from the link diagrams themselves, and we discuss what would be needed to close that gap

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Hashing Circuit Optimization for Arbitrary Qubit Connectivity Graphs Based on 1-Covering Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09134)
*Kamil Khadiev,Aliya Khadieva,Vadim Sagitov,Kamil Khasanov,Mansur Ziatdinov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算法，可将包含CNOT门序列的量子电路转换为适配任意量子计算机架构（具有有限量子比特连接性）的形式，并在量子指纹识别和量子有限自动机等应用中展示了该算法的有效性，实现了16%-17%的CNOT电路成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件存在量子比特间连接受限的问题（每个量子比特仅与少数其他量子比特连接），这要求电路必须进行转换以适应硬件拓扑结构。高效的电路综合问题是量子计算广泛应用的主要障碍之一，因此需要开发能够将量子电路映射到具有任意量子比特连接图约束的实际量子设备上的算法。

Method: 提出了一种电路转换算法，专门处理包含CNOT门序列的量子电路，使其适配具有量子比特连接图约束的量子设备架构。该算法在量子哈希算法（量子指纹识别算法）的背景下进行了演示，并应用于识别特定语言（如unary MOD_p语言和EQ_p语言）的量子有限自动机。该方法可处理类似于教科书量子傅里叶变换中常见的门序列模式。

Result: 在测试案例中，该算法实现了16%-17%的CNOT电路成本降低。算法成功地将量子指纹识别电路和量子有限自动机电路转换为适配受限量子硬件架构的形式，展示了在实际量子设备约束条件下的电路优化能力。

Conclusion: 该算法为解决量子电路在受限连接架构上的综合问题提供了有效方案，不仅在量子指纹识别应用中表现出色，还具有在更广泛的量子编译场景中应用的潜力。算法所实现的电路成本降低和对任意量子架构的适配能力，有助于推动量子计算的实际应用和普及。

Abstract: One of the obstacles to the widespread adoption of quantum computing is the problem of efficient circuit synthesis. Current quantum hardware has limited connections between qubits, with each qubit connected to only a few others. This means that the circuit has to be transformed to accommodate this. In this paper, we present an algorithm that converts a circuit containing a sequence of CNOT gates into a form that is suitable for arbitrary quantum computer architectures. Although we demonstrate the algorithm only in the context of quantum fingerprinting, similar gate sequences are prevalent in quantum algorithms; for instance, they are present in the textbook quantum Fourier transform. We present a quantum circuit implementation of the quantum hashing algorithm (quantum fingerprinting algorithm) for a quantum device with restrictions on the application of two-qubit gates that are expressed as a qubit connectivity graph. As an example of usage of the technique, we apply it to quantum finite automata recognizing the unary $MOD_p=\{a^\ell: \ell \bmod p=0\}$ language, and the $EQ_p=\{a^\ell b^r: \ell \equiv r \pmod p\}$ language. Given the enhancements that our algorithm provides~-- for instance, in one case it achieves a 16\%--17\% decrease in CNOT circuit cost~-- we believe it could also be useful in a broader quantum compilation context.

</details>


### [132] [Evidence for Counterfactual Violation of Local Conservation Laws in Quantum Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09205)
*Patrick Cameron,Francesco Di Colandrea,Filippo Cardano,Lorenzo Marrucci*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过光学涡旋实验首次提供了量子事件可能违反局域动量守恒定律的实验证据,基于超振荡现象,展示了一种不依赖于纠缠的新型量子非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理守恒定律(如能量和动量守恒)被普遍认为在时空中严格且局域地成立,但Aharonov、Popescu和Rohrlich基于超振荡现象的思想实验提出,单个量子事件(而非系综平均)可能偶尔违反局域守恒定律。该研究旨在为这一理论预测提供实验验证,探索量子力学中守恒定律的基本性质以及可能存在的新型量子非定域性。

Method: 研究者从光学涡旋核心附近的小型超振荡区域中提取光子,测量其平均横向动量。关键假设是提取机制不改变光子的平均横向动量。通过统计分析验证测量结果是否与局域动量守恒所隐含的一般界限相容。同时检测高横向动量光子的出现率,并与假设局域动量守恒的模型预测进行比较。利用后选择和反事实推理方法建立违反守恒定律的证据。

Result: 实验显示从超振荡区域提取的光子的平均横向动量在统计上与局域动量守恒隐含的界限不相容。高横向动量光子的检测率显著超过了假设局域动量守恒的模型预测值。这些结果提供了量子事件违反局域动量守恒的实验证据。由于这种违反只能通过反事实和后选择方式建立,因此不与相对论因果性冲突。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了单个量子事件可能违反局域动量守恒定律的实验证据,支持了APR基于超振荡现象的理论预测。这种违反通过反事实和后选择方式建立,不违背相对论因果性。研究结果可能代表了一种新型量子非定域性的首个实例,该非定域性不明确依赖于量子纠缠,为理解量子力学的基本性质和守恒定律在量子尺度上的适用性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Physical conservation laws, such as those of energy and momentum, are generally believed to hold exactly and locally in spacetime, including in quantum phenomena. Yet Aharonov, Popescu, and Rohrlich (APR) recently argued, on the basis of a thought experiment, that individual quantum events, unlike ensemble averages, may occasionally violate local conservation laws. Their argument relies on the wave phenomenon known as "superoscillations", which APR themselves discovered more than 30 years ago. Here we provide experimental evidence for such a violation. We extract photons from a small superoscillatory region near the core of an optical vortex and show that their mean transverse momentum is statistically incompatible with a general bound implied by local momentum conservation. The derivation of this bound requires only the theoretically well-supported assumption that the extraction mechanism does not alter the photons' mean transverse momentum. We also detect photons with high transverse momentum at a rate significantly exceeding that predicted by a model assuming local momentum conservation. Because this violation can be established only counterfactually and through postselection, it does not conflict with relativistic causality. Our results may represent the first example of a distinct form of quantum nonlocality that does not explicitly rely on entanglement.

</details>


### [133] [Squeezing for dispersive readout of NV magnetometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09212)
*A. M. Kozodaev,S. M. Drofa,P. G. Vilyuzhanina,A. Chernyavskiy,N. I. Salangin,A. N. Smolyaninov,S. Ya. Kilin,I. S. Cojocaru,S. V. Bolshedvorskii,V. V. Soshenko,F. Y. Khalili,A. V. Akimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文详细分析了基于氮空位中心的微波色散读出磁力计的实际噪声源和损耗机制,并证明利用微波场的压缩量子态可以在室温下提升器件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于金刚石氮空位中心的磁力计虽然发展迅速,但光学读出方案使系统设计复杂化。尽管已提出使用高精细度介质微腔进行微波场色散读出的无光学方案,但此前仅有基于散粒噪声的灵敏度估算,缺乏对实际噪声源和损耗的详细分析。

Method: 对基于高精细度微波介质腔的氮空位中心色散读出系统进行详细的噪声和损耗分析,包括各种实际相关的噪声源。进一步研究在探测微波场中引入压缩量子态的可行性,评估其对器件性能的改善效果。

Result: 提供了该类传感器系统中各种实际噪声源和损耗机制的详细分析结果。证明了即使在室温条件下,使用微波场的压缩量子态也能够有效提升磁力计的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过全面分析实际噪声和损耗因素,本研究为基于微波色散读出的氮空位中心磁力计提供了更realistic的性能评估。更重要的是,研究表明量子压缩技术可在室温下应用于此类器件以增强灵敏度,为实用化的高性能量子传感器开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Nitrogen vacancy centers in diamond have established themselves as good sensing element for various type of sensors. In particular magnetometers based on diamond impurities are quickly developing and are already on the market. Yet, optical readout in these systems complicates system design. Recently schemes of dispersive readout of nitrogen vacancy spin state using high finesse dielectric cavities for microwave field were proposed, which do not use the optical readout scheme. However, only shot noise based estimates were so far done for sensitivity of these devices. Here we provide detailed analysis of various practically relevant noise and loss sources for such a system. Furthermore, we consider the possibility of using the squeezing quantum state of the probing microwave field and show it allows to improve the device performance even at room temperature.

</details>


### [134] [A Heterogeneous Distributed Architecture for Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09215)
*John Stack,Sitong Liu,Abhinav Anand,Inder Monga,Yuan Liu,Erhan Saglamyurek,Pedro L. S. Lopes,Frank Mueller,Katherine Klymko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构分布式量子计算架构，通过将魔态核心与一维冷存储节点通道连接，实现了高效的费米子量子模拟。该架构在450逻辑量子比特的Fermi-Hubbard和稀疏SYK模型模拟中，相比同构架构显著降低了资源开销并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机的架构专门化和分布式设计虽能提升可扩展性，但会引入通信、路由和资源重复等大量开销。费米子量子模拟中产生的高度非局域泡利串操作对架构提出了特殊需求，需要设计能够高效处理这类并行非局域操作的专用架构。

Method: 设计了一种异构分布式架构，包含：(1)魔态核心(magic core)连接到可扩展存储系统；(2)存储系统由一维专用冷存储节点通道(lanes)组成；(3)支持对泡利串奇偶性的并行随机访问。该架构针对费米子量子模拟进行了优化，能够并行执行高度非局域的泡利串操作。在最多450个逻辑量子比特的Fermi-Hubbard和稀疏Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev(SYK)模型上进行容错动力学模拟评估。

Result: 对于450逻辑量子比特Fermi-Hubbard工作负载的一个Trotter步：(1)配备30个T态工厂的六通道系统，其壁钟时间约为具有4倍T态工厂数量、更高连接性和魔态注入点的同构分布式架构的1.4倍；(2)在T态工厂数量匹配的情况下，该架构速度提升约2倍。架构成功处理了两种互补的通信结构：Fermi-Hubbard的从局域到非局域的交互谱，以及稀疏SYK的高度非局域重叠泡利算符。

Conclusion: 提出的异构分布式量子架构通过专门化的冷存储通道设计和魔态核心组织，有效解决了费米子量子模拟中非局域泡利串操作的执行难题。相比传统同构架构，该方案在保持相近性能的同时大幅减少了T态工厂等关键资源需求(降至1/4)，并在相同资源条件下实现了2倍性能提升，为大规模容错量子计算提供了资源高效的架构方案。

Abstract: Architectural specialization and distribution can help scale fault-tolerant quantum computers, but may also introduce substantial overheads from communication, routing, and resource duplication. We introduce a heterogeneous distributed architecture in which a magic core is connected to an extensible storage system composed of one-dimensional lanes of specialized cold-storage nodes. The storage system supports parallel random access to Pauli string parities. This organization is particularly well suited to fermionic quantum simulation, enabling parallel execution of the highly non-local Pauli strings arising from these systems. We evaluate the architecture on fault-tolerant simulations of the dynamics of the Fermi-Hubbard and sparse Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) models on systems of up to 450 logical qubits. These workloads exhibit complementary communication structures: Fermi-Hubbard produces a spectrum of interactions from local to non-local shaped by lattice geometry, whereas sparse SYK produces highly non-local and overlapping Pauli operators. For a Trotter step of a 450-logical-qubit Fermi-Hubbard workload, a six-lane system with 30 T-state factories is within approximately $1.4\times$ the wall-clock time of a homogeneous distributed architecture with 4 times as many T-state factories and substantially greater connectivity and sites for injecting magic. For matched T-factory counts, our architecture is $\sim 2\times$ faster.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum uncertainty in a macroscopic domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09262)
*Othman Q. Malhas,Bacim Alali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过经典模型论构造了偏布尔代数的表示,在保持经典命题逻辑的前提下实现了类量子不确定性的形式化描述,并利用KS-可着色性、测量反作用和具体反例展示了这些量子现象源于命题与模型的组织结构而非非经典逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学的不确定性和测量反作用等现象通常被认为需要放弃经典逻辑才能描述。本文旨在证明这些类量子现象可以在经典命题逻辑框架内通过模型论方法来表达,从而揭示这些现象的本质来源于命题和模型的结构组织,而非逻辑系统本身的非经典性质。

Method: 1) 基于偏布尔代数(V,Π)构造一致性理论Tg,其核心(初始公式等价类的有序集)与偏布尔代数同构;2) 将模型刻画为向上闭簇(upward-closed clusters),建立KS-可着色性的模型论表述;3) 通过预框架(pre-frame)中局部可容许原子结果的数量定义有限谱可观测量的不确定性;4) 引入测量更新假设来模拟测量不兼容可观测量导致锐度破坏的反作用效应;5) 构造具体例子:12顶点偏布尔代数(KS-可着色)和140顶点四维偏布尔代数(通过奇偶性论证证明非KS-可着色,同构于Peres 24射线配置)。

Result: 1) 成功建立了n维偏布尔代数KS-可着色性的模型论判据:当且仅当诱导理论存在与每个预框架在一个原初公式处相交的模型;2) 通过单点预框架条件刻画了无色散模型;3) 有限例子展示了测量不兼容可观测量如何破坏锐度,实现了反作用的模型论形式;4) 构造的140顶点四维偏布尔代数被证明为非KS-可着色,其关联结构同构于R⁴中的Peres 24射线、24基配置;5) 宏观解释表明这些现象源于命题和模型的组织而非非经典推演;6) 建立了特定模型与纯态、密度算符的概率为1命题之间的联系。

Conclusion: 类量子的不确定性、测量反作用和KS不可着色性等现象可以在保持经典命题逻辑的框架内通过模型论方法实现,这些现象的根源在于命题与模型的结构组织方式而非逻辑系统的非经典性。虽然某些确定性模型与量子纯态相关联,但它们并不能完全决定量子态本身,这为理解量子现象的经典逻辑基础提供了新视角。

Abstract: We develop a classical model-theoretic representation of partial Boolean algebras and use it to formulate quantum-like uncertainty without abandoning classical propositional logic. Given a surjection from the initial formulæ of a propositional language onto a partial Boolean algebra $(\mathcal V,Π)$, we construct a consistent theory $\mathcal T_g$ whose core---the ordered set of equivalence classes of initial formulæ---is isomorphic to $(\mathcal V,Π)$. Its models are characterized as upward-closed clusters, yielding a model-theoretic formulation of KS-colourability: an $n$-dimensional partial Boolean algebra is KS-colourable exactly when the induced theory has a model meeting every pre-frame in one primitive formula.
  For finite-spectrum observables, uncertainty is defined by the number of locally admissible atomic outcomes. Dispersion-free models are characterized by the singleton pre-frame condition. With an additional measurement-update postulate, a finite example shows how measurement of an incompatible observable can destroy sharpness, providing a model-theoretic form of back-action. A $12$-vertex partial Boolean algebra is KS-colourable, whereas a rigorously constructed $140$-vertex, four-dimensional partial Boolean algebra is not, as shown by a parity argument; its incidence structure is isomorphic to the Peres $24$-ray, $24$-basis configuration in $\mathbb R^4$. Macroscopic interpretations show that these phenomena arise from the organization of propositions and models rather than from nonclassical deduction. Finally, we relate certain models to the probability-one propositions of pure states and density operators, while emphasizing that such certainty models do not determine the full quantum state.

</details>


### [136] [High-fidelity controlled-phase gates for distinguishable quantum walkers via extended interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09301)
*Gaia Forghieri,Matteo G. A. Paris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用相互作用粒子在双轨编码中通过量子行走实现受控相位门，证明了扩展相互作用范围可使可区分粒子达到与不可区分粒子相当的性能，并且避免了路由所需的额外单粒子门，使可区分粒子方案在实际应用中更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前方案使用不可区分粒子（玻色子或费米子）在平面波散射中实现理想保真度，但实际实现需要有限尺寸波包和单粒子门路由，这会引入不可避免的保真度损失。研究动机是探索能否通过可区分粒子实现高保真度的受控相位门，从而避免不可区分粒子方案中额外的路由门带来的保真度损失。

Method: 1) 将相互作用范围从近邻扩展到多近邻项，以获得对散射势的充分控制；2) 针对有限高斯波包，推导了门保真度关于传输系数幅度和相位导数的解析近似表达式；3) 比较可区分粒子散射与不可区分粒子（玻色子/费米子）散射的性能；4) 分析整体架构中相互作用引起的保真度损失与路由门引起的保真度损失。

Result: 1) 扩展相互作用范围后，可区分粒子散射能够达到与理想玻色子/费米子性能相匹配的效果；2) 虽然单独的可区分粒子散射保真度略低于不可区分粒子情况，但避免了不可区分粒子方案所需的额外单粒子路由门；3) 路由不可区分粒子所需的绕行门引入的保真度损失比相互作用引起的损失大约一个数量级；4) 可区分粒子方法在实际实现中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 多近邻相互作用可作为连续时间量子行走中量子信息处理的有效工具。尽管可区分粒子散射本身保真度略低，但由于其整体架构避免了不可区分粒子方案中高损耗的路由门，使得可区分粒子方案在实际量子门实现中更具优势。研究为有限尺寸系统中优化门保真度提供了定量指导原则。

Abstract: We investigate the implementation of controlled-phase (CP) gates with quantum walks in a dual-rail encoding through the use of interacting particles. While previous proposals have focused on indistinguishable particles (bosons or fermions) to achieve unitary fidelity for plane-wave scattering, practical implementations require finite-size wavepackets and routing through single-particle gates, both factors that introduce unavoidable fidelity losses. We show that extending the interaction range beyond on-site or first-neighbor terms provides sufficient control over the scattering potential to engineer CP gates with distinguishable particles that match the ideal bosonic/fermionic performance. For finite Gaussian wavepackets, we derive analytical approximations for the gate fidelity in terms of the transmission coefficient's magnitude and phase derivatives. We find that while distinguishable-particle scattering alone exhibits slightly lower fidelity than the indistinguishable case, the overall architecture that we propose avoids the additional single-particle gates required for routing indistinguishable particles. The roundabout gates needed for the latter indeed introduce fidelity losses approximately one order of magnitude larger than the interaction-induced losses, making the distinguishable-particle approach competitive for practical implementations. Our results establish multi-neighbor interactions as a tool for quantum information processing with continuous-time quantum walks and provide quantitative guidelines for optimizing gate fidelities in finite-size systems.

</details>


### [137] [A high-performance quantum pulse gate in thin-film lithium niobate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09346)
*Silia Babel,Alejandra Alarcón,Laura Serino,Christian Golla,Laura Bollmers,Sebastian Lengeling,Jiayu Yang,Bernhard Reineke,Christof Eigner,Benjamin Brecht,Marko Lončar,Laura Padberg,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在薄膜铌酸锂平台上实现了高性能量子脉冲门(QPG),通过优化波导几何结构实现0型和频产生,将归一化转换效率提升三个数量级至1810 W⁻¹cm⁻²,同时保持96.8%的时间模式选择性,为光子量子技术中的实用化QPG奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子脉冲门存在两个主要限制:1) 涉及光场的波长和偏振组合受限;2) 归一化转换效率低。这些限制阻碍了QPG在光子量子技术中的广泛应用。需要开发新平台来突破这些性能瓶颈,实现高效率、高选择性的量子光时间模式操控。

Method: 在薄膜铌酸锂平台上设计并制造量子脉冲门:1) 设计特定波导几何结构以提供高效0型和频产生所需的色散特性;2) 通过相位匹配强度映射验证设计;3) 利用薄膜铌酸锂的强光场约束特性提升转换效率;4) 测量时间模式选择性和内部转换效率来评估QPG性能。

Result: 实现了(96.8±1.7)%的时间模式选择性,与现有QPG性能相当;在仅20mW泵浦功率下达到(89.6±0.1)%的内部转换效率;归一化转换效率下限估计为(1810±10) W⁻¹cm⁻²,比以往QPG高出三个数量级;成功验证了薄膜铌酸锂平台的相位匹配特性和高质量QPG操作。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了薄膜铌酸锂作为高性能量子脉冲门理想平台的地位,通过三个数量级的转换效率提升和优异的模式选择性,为光子量子技术中实用化QPG的发展迈出了重要一步,有望推动量子光时间模式操控在量子信息处理中的广泛应用。

Abstract: In this work, we demonstrate a quantum pulse gate (QPG) in thin-film lithium niobate. QPGs enable the selective manipulation and detection of temporal modes of quantum light and form the basis of numerous applications in photonic quantum technologies. To date, their widespread adaption is held back by two main limitations: restricted wavelength and polarization combinations of the involved fields and low normalized conversion efficiencies. We overcome these limitations through developing a QPG in thin-film lithium niobate. We design a waveguide geometry that provides the required dispersion properties for a highly efficient type-0 sum-frequency generation.We verify our design through mapping of the phase matching intensity, and demonstrate high-quality QPG operation by measuring a temporal-mode selectivity of (96.8$\pm$1.7)% on par with existing QPGs. Thanks to the strong confinement in thin-film lithium niobate, we succeed in demonstrating an internal conversion efficiency of (89.6$\pm$0.1)% for a pump power of only 20mW in front of our sample. This yields a lower-bound estimate for the normalized conversion efficiency of (1810$\pm$10)$\mathrm{W}^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$, three orders of magnitude higher than in previous QPGs. Our results establish thin-film lithium niobate as ideal platform for high-performance QPGs and are a major step towards practical QPGs for photonic quantum technologies.

</details>


### [138] [Birefringent Biomineral Microcarriers Stabilise Multimodal Nanodiamond Quantum Sensing in Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09434)
*T. Amro,M. Attrash,A. Droby,A. Ushkov,R. Malkinson,P. Penshin,A. Hen,V. Bobrovs,P. Ginzburg,H. Barhum,N. Bar-Gill*

TL;DR: 本文报告了一种新型量子传感平台,通过将40纳米氮空位金刚石纳米颗粒组装在多孔双折射的碳酸钙微球表面,实现了在复杂生物液体中稳定的磁场、浓度和pH的多模态量子传感,克服了传统移动纳米金刚石量子传感器在液体环境中面临的布朗旋转、光子收集不稳定和光阱扰动等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动纳米金刚石量子传感器在液体环境中的应用受到布朗旋转、光子收集效率不稳定以及光学捕获引入的扰动等问题的严重限制。为解决这些问题,需要开发一种能够提供取向稳定、光学操控、磁传感和界面化学响应的集成平台,以实现在复杂生物液体中的多模态量子传感。

Method: 将40纳米氮空位(NV)金刚石纳米颗粒组装在多孔、双折射的碳酸钙(方解石变体)微球表面,构建杂化量子传感平台。利用976纳米波长光阱进行光学捕获,通过塞曼分裂共振实现磁场传感,利用碳酸盐界面的质子活性进行浓度和pH传感。建立了大正则系综电荷调控模型,将质子化学势与NV光谱窗口内的界面开关动力学耦合,以描述介质依赖的动态质子转导机制。

Result: 在976纳米、0.8瓦光阱下,杂化体系保持自旋共振和纵向弛豫特性,共振对比度变化小于7%,共振中心频移约1 MHz。在0至0.8毫特斯拉范围内实现磁场传感,响应灵敏度为78-144微特斯拉/√Hz。在缓冲细胞培养基中,10.7微摩尔名义质子当量剂量使T1从23.4±2.3微秒缩短至9.0±1.2微秒,在DMEM培养基中浓度灵敏度为6.46微摩尔/√Hz,pH灵敏度为6.54毫pH/√Hz。相比之下,乙醇中500倍更高的质子剂量产生的自旋响应显著较弱,突显了碳酸盐界面的关键作用。

Conclusion: 通过将氮空位纳米金刚石与碳酸钙微球杂化,成功构建了一个集成取向稳定、光学操控、磁场传感和界面化学响应功能的多模态量子传感平台。碳酸盐富集界面在质子活性转导中发挥关键作用,实现了在复杂生物液体中的稳定传感。该平台为生物环境中的量子传感应用提供了通用且多功能的解决方案,特别是在细胞培养等复杂化学环境中的磁场、浓度和pH多参数同步监测。

Abstract: Mobile nanodiamond quantum sensors in liquids are limited by Brownian rotation, variable photon collection, and perturbations introduced by optical trapping. Here, we assemble 40-nm nitrogen-vacancy nanodiamonds at the surface of porous, birefringent vaterite microspherulites. This carbonate biomineral provides a polarization-addressable body frame, anisotropic emission redistribution, and a proton-active thermodynamic interface. Under 976-nm optical trapping, the hybrids retain their spin resonance and longitudinal relaxation, with the resonance contrast varying by less than 7 percent and the resonance centre shifting by approximately 1 MHz at 0.8 W. Zeeman-split resonances enable magnetic-field sensing from 0 to 0.8 mT, with a response metric of 78-144 microT per square root Hz. In buffered cell-culture medium, a 10.7 microM nominal proton-equivalent dose shortens T1 from 23.4 +/- 2.3 to 9.0 +/- 1.2 microseconds, corresponding to concentration and pH sensitivities of 6.46 microM per square root Hz and 6.54 mpH per square root Hz, respectively, in DMEM. By contrast, a 500-fold higher proton dose in ethanol produces a substantially weaker spin response, highlighting the role of the carbonate-rich interface. We develop a grand-canonical charge-regulation model coupling proton chemical potential to interfacial switching kinetics within the NV spectral window, capturing medium-dependent dynamic proton transduction. By integrating orientation stabilisation, optical manipulation, magnetic sensing, and interfacial chemical response, this carbonate-rich hybrid platform establishes a versatile approach to multimodal quantum sensing in complex biological liquids.

</details>


### [139] [A non-Markovian approach to spin-phonon coherence and the breakdown of the Markovian approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09455)
*Mohamed Belhassen,Tim Schröder,Gregor Pieplow*
